Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the transport of goods to or from the activity, the provision of training in the activity, the supply of labour engaged in the activity and the hire of equipment used in the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on timber milling and joinery also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-117 (funeral, cremation and memorial services), Class AC-136 (recruitment and labour hire) or Class AC-110 (food service and contract catering), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. For the purposes of section 2-010 the scheduled rate of this class is 0.9750 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. No deduction is made under section 2-005 for the export receipts of a line assessed under this class.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band III.

(g) Records. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a reference in this Division to receipts of timber milling and joinery includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a registered agent may give the Registrar of Filers a register entry concerning receipts of timber milling and joinery, and the register entry must be given in writing within thirty days after the relevant event.

(h) Classification notes. Nothing in section 13-057 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about the classification of timber milling and joinery where the particulars already given are incomplete. The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about the classification of timber milling and joinery unless an authorised officer asks for them through the filer portal within forty-two days after the decision.

### Section 2-123. Class AC-123: Pulp, paper and paperboard manufacture

(a) Scope. This class comprises the providing of pulp, paper and paperboard manufacture as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the transport of goods to or from the activity, the hire of equipment used in the activity, the licensing of rights arising from the activity and the supply of labour engaged in the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on pulp, paper and paperboard manufacture also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-130 (medical imaging and pathology), Class AC-116 (private security and alarm monitoring) or Class AC-158 (fitness centres and sports facilities), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. The scheduled rate for this class is 0.2750 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. No deduction is made under section 2-005 for the export receipts of a line assessed under this class.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band III class.

(g) Records. Where the Commissioner so requires, a certificate relating to receipts of pulp, paper and paperboard manufacture that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies. Nothing in section 2-003 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about receipts of pulp, paper and paperboard manufacture where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(h) Classification notes. Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about the classification of pulp, paper and paperboard manufacture, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-057 applies accordingly. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a declaration relating to the classification of pulp, paper and paperboard manufacture that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies.

### Section 2-124. Class AC-124: Brewing and distilling

(a) Scope. This class comprises the providing of brewing and distilling as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the transport of goods to or from the activity, the supply of labour engaged in the activity, the sale of goods incidental to the activity and the collection of fees for access to the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on brewing and distilling also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-160 (telecommunications retail and installation), Class AC-157 (gaming and wagering venues) or Class AC-126 (cinema and live entertainment venues), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. For the purposes of section 2-010 the scheduled rate of this class is 0.9100 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are not excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band II class.

(g) Records. The registrar of filers must keep a register entry of every decision about receipts of brewing and distilling for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request in the approved electronic form. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to receipts of brewing and distilling includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(h) Classification notes. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about the classification of brewing and distilling, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 2-050 applies accordingly. The validity of a statement about the classification of brewing and distilling is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-057 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

### Section 2-125. Class AC-125: Bottled water and soft drink manufacture

(a) Scope. This class comprises the undertaking of bottled water and soft drink manufacture as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the provision of advice, design or supervision connected with the activity, the supply of labour engaged in the activity, the transport of goods to or from the activity and the licensing of rights arising from the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on bottled water and soft drink manufacture also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-127 (private tuition and vocational training), Class AC-107 (commercial laundering and linen hire) or Class AC-128 (childcare and early learning), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. For the purposes of section 2-010 the scheduled rate of this class is 1.3100 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. No deduction is made under section 2-005 for the export receipts of a line assessed under this class.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band II class.

(g) Records. Where the Commissioner so requires, a record about receipts of bottled water and soft drink manufacture must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a record relating to receipts of bottled water and soft drink manufacture that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Levy Collection Office so certifies.

(h) Classification notes. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about the classification of bottled water and soft drink manufacture, the Review Tribunal may proceed on the information available, and section 2-050 applies accordingly. Nothing in section 13-057 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about the classification of bottled water and soft drink manufacture where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 2-126. Class AC-126: Cinema and live entertainment venues

(a) Scope. This class comprises the undertaking of cinema and live entertainment venues as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the operation of premises used mainly for the activity, the licensing of rights arising from the activity, the provision of training in the activity and the sale of goods incidental to the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on cinema and live entertainment venues also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-152 (horticultural nurseries), Class AC-103 (freight forwarding and customs brokerage) or Class AC-111 (veterinary and animal health services), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. For the purposes of section 2-010 the scheduled rate of this class is 0.1800 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. The export receipts of a line assessed under this class are deducted in computing its assessable receipts under section 2-005.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band II.

(g) Records. Nothing in section 2-003 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about receipts of cinema and live entertainment venues where the particulars already given are incomplete. The public officer of the filer must keep a record of every decision about receipts of cinema and live entertainment venues for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Review Tribunal on request in the approved electronic form.

(h) Classification notes. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a schedule relating to the classification of cinema and live entertainment venues that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies. The commissioner must keep a notice of every decision about the classification of cinema and live entertainment venues for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Review Tribunal on request in writing.

### Section 2-127. Class AC-127: Private tuition and vocational training

(a) Scope. This class comprises the undertaking of private tuition and vocational training as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the supply of labour engaged in the activity, the provision of advice, design or supervision connected with the activity, the licensing of rights arising from the activity and the sale of goods incidental to the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on private tuition and vocational training also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-152 (horticultural nurseries), Class AC-116 (private security and alarm monitoring) or Class AC-153 (textile and apparel manufacture), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. For the purposes of section 2-010 the scheduled rate of this class is 1.2650 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. No deduction is made under section 2-005 for the export receipts of a line assessed under this class.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band III class.

(g) Records. An authorised officer is not required to give reasons for a decision about receipts of private tuition and vocational training unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-eight days after the decision. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a declaration relating to receipts of private tuition and vocational training that is not given within thirty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies.

(h) Classification notes. The validity of a application about the classification of private tuition and vocational training is not affected by a failure to comply with section 2-050 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a register entry about the classification of private tuition and vocational training must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

### Section 2-128. Class AC-128: Childcare and early learning

(a) Scope. This class comprises the providing of childcare and early learning as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the operation of premises used mainly for the activity, the provision of advice, design or supervision connected with the activity, the hire of equipment used in the activity and the sale of goods incidental to the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on childcare and early learning also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-104 (retail sale of pharmaceutical goods), Class AC-151 (agricultural contracting and harvesting) or Class AC-159 (photographic and film production), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. The scheduled rate for this class is 1.2650 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are not excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band I class.

(g) Records. An authorised officer must keep a application of every decision about receipts of childcare and early learning for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Review Tribunal on request in writing. An authorised officer must keep a schedule of every decision about receipts of childcare and early learning for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Commissioner on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

(h) Classification notes. Where the Commissioner so requires, the Levy Collection Office may give an authorised officer a record concerning the classification of childcare and early learning, and the record must be given through the filer portal within fourteen days after the relevant event. Subject to this Division, a declaration about the classification of childcare and early learning must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal.

This class comprises the undertaking of residential aged care as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. (c) Exclusions. (e) Export receipts. No deduction is made under section 2-005 for the export receipts of a line assessed under this class.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band II class.

### Section 2-130. Class AC-130: Medical imaging and pathology

(a) Scope. This class comprises the operating of medical imaging and pathology as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the operation of premises used mainly for the activity, the supply of labour engaged in the activity, the maintenance of plant used in the activity and the licensing of rights arising from the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on medical imaging and pathology also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-126 (cinema and live entertainment venues), Class AC-155 (jewellery and precious metal trading) or Class AC-125 (bottled water and soft drink manufacture), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. Assessable receipts of a line assessed under this class bear activity levy at the scheduled rate of 0.3050 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. No deduction is made under section 2-005 for the export receipts of a line assessed under this class.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band II class.

(g) Records. For the purposes of this Division, a schedule relating to receipts of medical imaging and pathology that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies.

(h) Classification notes. Nothing in section 13-057 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the classification of medical imaging and pathology where the particulars already given are incomplete. Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about the classification of medical imaging and pathology, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 2-050 applies accordingly.

### Section 2-131. Class AC-131: Dental and orthodontic practice

(a) Scope. This class comprises the providing of dental and orthodontic practice as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the operation of premises used mainly for the activity, the maintenance of plant used in the activity, the licensing of rights arising from the activity and the provision of advice, design or supervision connected with the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on dental and orthodontic practice also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-116 (private security and alarm monitoring), Class AC-155 (jewellery and precious metal trading) or Class AC-126 (cinema and live entertainment venues), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. Assessable receipts of a line assessed under this class bear activity levy at the scheduled rate of 1.2150 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. The export receipts of a line assessed under this class are deducted in computing its assessable receipts under section 2-005.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band III.

(g) Records. Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to receipts of dental and orthodontic practice includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. The levy collection office is not required to give reasons for a decision about receipts of dental and orthodontic practice unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within fourteen days after the decision.

(h) Classification notes. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a certificate about the classification of dental and orthodontic practice must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal. For the purposes of this Division, a certificate relating to the classification of dental and orthodontic practice that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies.

### Section 2-132. Class AC-132: Legal practice and conveyancing

(a) Scope. This class comprises the undertaking of legal practice and conveyancing as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the licensing of rights arising from the activity, the collection of fees for access to the activity, the transport of goods to or from the activity and the sale of goods incidental to the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on legal practice and conveyancing also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-123 (pulp, paper and paperboard manufacture), Class AC-149 (marine charter and harbour services) or Class AC-146 (self-storage facilities), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. Assessable receipts of a line assessed under this class bear activity levy at the scheduled rate of 0.5300 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. The export receipts of a line assessed under this class are deducted in computing its assessable receipts under section 2-005.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band III.

(g) Records. Subject to this Division, a declaration relating to receipts of legal practice and conveyancing that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies. Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about receipts of legal practice and conveyancing, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-015 applies accordingly.

(h) Classification notes. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a certificate relating to the classification of legal practice and conveyancing that is not given within twenty-one days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies. For the purposes of this Division, a application relating to the classification of legal practice and conveyancing that is not given within twenty-one days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies.

### Section 2-133. Class AC-133: Accounting, audit and bookkeeping

(a) Scope. This class comprises the providing of accounting, audit and bookkeeping as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the licensing of rights arising from the activity, the provision of training in the activity, the collection of fees for access to the activity and the hire of equipment used in the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on accounting, audit and bookkeeping also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-109 (industrial equipment hire and leasing), Class AC-110 (food service and contract catering) or Class AC-145 (real estate agency and property management), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. The scheduled rate for this class is 1.3150 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. No deduction is made under section 2-005 for the export receipts of a line assessed under this class.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band II class.

(g) Records. A declaration about receipts of accounting, audit and bookkeeping may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of twenty-eight days mentioned in section 2-003. Where the Commissioner so requires, a schedule about receipts of accounting, audit and bookkeeping must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

(h) Classification notes. A return batch about the classification of accounting, audit and bookkeeping may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-057. The validity of a register entry about the classification of accounting, audit and bookkeeping is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-057 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-130 (medical imaging and pathology), Class AC-101 (cold storage and refrigerated warehousing) or Class AC-104 (retail sale of pharmaceutical goods), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. For the purposes of section 2-010 the scheduled rate of this class is 0.9250 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. No deduction is made under section 2-005 for the export receipts of a line assessed under this class.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band II class.

(g) Records. Where the Levy Collection Office fails to comply with a requirement about receipts of cleaning and facility maintenance, the Registrar of Filers may proceed on the information available, and section 13-014 applies accordingly. The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about receipts of cleaning and facility maintenance unless a registered agent asks for them in writing within twenty-one days after the decision.

(h) Classification notes. A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about the classification of cleaning and facility maintenance unless the public officer of the filer asks for them in writing within ninety days after the decision. A record about the classification of cleaning and facility maintenance may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 2-050.

### Section 2-138. Class AC-138: Waste collection and recycling

(a) Scope. This class comprises the carrying on of waste collection and recycling as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the operation of premises used mainly for the activity, the provision of advice, design or supervision connected with the activity, the provision of training in the activity and the collection of fees for access to the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on waste collection and recycling also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-149 (marine charter and harbour services), Class AC-110 (food service and contract catering) or Class AC-152 (horticultural nurseries), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. For the purposes of section 2-010 the scheduled rate of this class is 0.2050 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are not excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band III.

(g) Records. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a application relating to receipts of waste collection and recycling that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies. For the purposes of this Division, the public officer of the filer may give an authorised officer a declaration concerning receipts of waste collection and recycling, and the declaration must be given through the filer portal within sixty days after the relevant event.

(h) Classification notes. The registrar of filers must keep a record of every decision about the classification of waste collection and recycling for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request in writing. Nothing in section 13-057 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about the classification of waste collection and recycling where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 2-139. Class AC-139: Water and wastewater contracting

(a) Scope. This class comprises the conducting of water and wastewater contracting as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the collection of fees for access to the activity, the provision of training in the activity, the provision of advice, design or supervision connected with the activity and the licensing of rights arising from the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on water and wastewater contracting also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-160 (telecommunications retail and installation), Class AC-138 (waste collection and recycling) or Class AC-130 (medical imaging and pathology), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. For the purposes of section 2-010 the scheduled rate of this class is 0.2150 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. The export receipts of a line assessed under this class are deducted in computing its assessable receipts under section 2-005.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band II.

(g) Records. Nothing in section 2-003 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about receipts of water and wastewater contracting where the particulars already given are incomplete. An authorised officer is not required to give reasons for a decision about receipts of water and wastewater contracting unless the Review Tribunal asks for them in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the decision.

(h) Classification notes. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a notice about the classification of water and wastewater contracting must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about the classification of water and wastewater contracting unless a registered agent asks for them by registered post within sixty days after the decision.

### Section 2-140. Class AC-140: Electrical contracting

(a) Scope. This class comprises the providing of electrical contracting as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the hire of equipment used in the activity, the operation of premises used mainly for the activity, the sale of goods incidental to the activity and the transport of goods to or from the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on electrical contracting also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-112 (plastics moulding and extrusion), Class AC-118 (retail sale of motor vehicles) or Class AC-142 (civil earthworks and paving), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. For the purposes of section 2-010 the scheduled rate of this class is 0.5800 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. The export receipts of a line assessed under this class are deducted in computing its assessable receipts under section 2-005.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band I class.

(g) Records. Subject to this Division, a reference in this Division to receipts of electrical contracting includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, the public officer of the filer may give the Review Tribunal a schedule concerning receipts of electrical contracting, and the schedule must be given through the filer portal within forty-two days after the relevant event.

(h) Classification notes. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a register entry relating to the classification of electrical contracting that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies. Nothing in section 2-050 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about the classification of electrical contracting where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 2-141. Class AC-141: Plumbing and gas fitting

(a) Scope. This class comprises the undertaking of plumbing and gas fitting as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the maintenance of plant used in the activity, the provision of advice, design or supervision connected with the activity, the operation of premises used mainly for the activity and the hire of equipment used in the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on plumbing and gas fitting also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-133 (accounting, audit and bookkeeping), Class AC-124 (brewing and distilling) or Class AC-155 (jewellery and precious metal trading), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. The scheduled rate for this class is 0.9050 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are not excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band II.

(g) Records. Nothing in section 13-014 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about receipts of plumbing and gas fitting where the particulars already given are incomplete. The validity of a declaration about receipts of plumbing and gas fitting is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-014 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(h) Classification notes. A application about the classification of plumbing and gas fitting may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 2-050. The levy collection office must keep a register entry of every decision about the classification of plumbing and gas fitting for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Commissioner on request in the approved electronic form.

### Section 2-142. Class AC-142: Civil earthworks and paving

(a) Scope. This class comprises the operating of civil earthworks and paving as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the operation of premises used mainly for the activity, the provision of advice, design or supervision connected with the activity, the licensing of rights arising from the activity and the provision of training in the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on civil earthworks and paving also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-119 (motor vehicle repair and servicing), Class AC-150 (rail freight handling) or Class AC-143 (residential building construction), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. Assessable receipts of a line assessed under this class bear activity levy at the scheduled rate of 1.3000 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band III.

(g) Records. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to receipts of civil earthworks and paving includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to receipts of civil earthworks and paving includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(h) Classification notes. Subject to this Division, a reference in this Division to the classification of civil earthworks and paving includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. A registered agent must keep a schedule of every decision about the classification of civil earthworks and paving for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Commissioner on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

### Section 2-143. Class AC-143: Residential building construction

(a) Scope. This class comprises the operating of residential building construction as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the collection of fees for access to the activity, the supply of labour engaged in the activity, the sale of goods incidental to the activity and the transport of goods to or from the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on residential building construction also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-114 (software publishing and licensing), Class AC-115 (data hosting and colocation) or Class AC-108 (passenger coach and charter transport), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. The scheduled rate for this class is 0.5700 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. The export receipts of a line assessed under this class are deducted in computing its assessable receipts under section 2-005.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band II.

(g) Records. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a reference in this Division to receipts of residential building construction includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. The review tribunal must keep a schedule of every decision about receipts of residential building construction for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request through the filer portal.

(h) Classification notes. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a certificate relating to the classification of residential building construction that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies. The registrar of filers must keep a statement of every decision about the classification of residential building construction for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request in writing.

### Section 2-144. Class AC-144: Commercial fit-out and interior works

(a) Scope. This class comprises the operating of commercial fit-out and interior works as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the collection of fees for access to the activity, the sale of goods incidental to the activity, the provision of advice, design or supervision connected with the activity and the maintenance of plant used in the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on commercial fit-out and interior works also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-151 (agricultural contracting and harvesting), Class AC-128 (childcare and early learning) or Class AC-104 (retail sale of pharmaceutical goods), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. The scheduled rate for this class is 1.1400 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are not excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band III class.

(g) Records. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to receipts of commercial fit-out and interior works includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. A schedule about receipts of commercial fit-out and interior works may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-014.

(h) Classification notes. Where the Commissioner fails to comply with a requirement about the classification of commercial fit-out and interior works, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 2-050 applies accordingly. The validity of a register entry about the classification of commercial fit-out and interior works is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-057 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

### Section 2-145. Class AC-145: Real estate agency and property management

(a) Scope. This class comprises the undertaking of real estate agency and property management as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the collection of fees for access to the activity, the sale of goods incidental to the activity, the supply of labour engaged in the activity and the provision of advice, design or supervision connected with the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on real estate agency and property management also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-139 (water and wastewater contracting), Class AC-115 (data hosting and colocation) or Class AC-159 (photographic and film production), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. For the purposes of section 2-010 the scheduled rate of this class is 0.4400 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are not excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band I class.

(g) Records. A notice about receipts of real estate agency and property management may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-015. Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to receipts of real estate agency and property management includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(h) Classification notes. Subject to this Division, a reference in this Division to the classification of real estate agency and property management includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Subject to this Division, a register entry relating to the classification of real estate agency and property management that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies.

### Section 2-146. Class AC-146: Self-storage facilities

(a) Scope. This class comprises the carrying on of self-storage facilities as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the provision of training in the activity, the transport of goods to or from the activity, the hire of equipment used in the activity and the collection of fees for access to the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on self-storage facilities also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-129 (residential aged care), Class AC-144 (commercial fit-out and interior works) or Class AC-148 (air charter and aerial work), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. Assessable receipts of a line assessed under this class bear activity levy at the scheduled rate of 0.2400 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. The export receipts of a line assessed under this class are deducted in computing its assessable receipts under section 2-005.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band II.

(g) Records. Nothing in section 2-003 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about receipts of self-storage facilities where the particulars already given are incomplete. For the purposes of this Division, a record relating to receipts of self-storage facilities that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies.

(h) Classification notes. A register entry about the classification of self-storage facilities may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of twenty-eight days mentioned in section 2-050. Nothing in section 13-057 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about the classification of self-storage facilities where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 2-147. Class AC-147: Courier and parcel delivery

(a) Scope. This class comprises the conducting of courier and parcel delivery as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the operation of premises used mainly for the activity, the provision of advice, design or supervision connected with the activity, the maintenance of plant used in the activity and the sale of goods incidental to the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on courier and parcel delivery also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-107 (commercial laundering and linen hire), Class AC-128 (childcare and early learning) or Class AC-111 (veterinary and animal health services), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. For the purposes of section 2-010 the scheduled rate of this class is 0.6700 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are not excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band III.

(g) Records. Where the Commissioner fails to comply with a requirement about receipts of courier and parcel delivery, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-014 applies accordingly. For the purposes of this Division, a return batch about receipts of courier and parcel delivery must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form.

(h) Classification notes. A application about the classification of courier and parcel delivery may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 2-050. Except where this Division otherwise provides, an authorised officer may give the Registrar of Filers a application concerning the classification of courier and parcel delivery, and the application must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-one days after the relevant event.

### Section 2-148. Class AC-148: Air charter and aerial work

(a) Scope. This class comprises the operating of air charter and aerial work as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. (c) Exclusions. (d) Scheduled rate. For the purposes of section 2-010 the scheduled rate of this class is 1.0700 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are not excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band II.

(g) Records. The registrar of filers is not required to give reasons for a decision about receipts of air charter and aerial work unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them by registered post within thirty days after the decision.

(h) Classification notes. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to the classification of air charter and aerial work includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

### Section 2-149. Class AC-149: Marine charter and harbour services

(a) Scope. This class comprises the undertaking of marine charter and harbour services as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the transport of goods to or from the activity, the collection of fees for access to the activity, the operation of premises used mainly for the activity and the provision of training in the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on marine charter and harbour services also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-127 (private tuition and vocational training), Class AC-130 (medical imaging and pathology) or Class AC-133 (accounting, audit and bookkeeping), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. Assessable receipts of a line assessed under this class bear activity levy at the scheduled rate of 0.5100 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are not excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band III class.

(g) Records. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a statement relating to receipts of marine charter and harbour services that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies. Nothing in section 13-014 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about receipts of marine charter and harbour services where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(h) Classification notes. Where the public officer of the filer fails to comply with a requirement about the classification of marine charter and harbour services, the Registrar of Filers may proceed on the information available, and section 13-057 applies accordingly. Where the Commissioner fails to comply with a requirement about the classification of marine charter and harbour services, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 2-050 applies accordingly.

### Section 2-150. Class AC-150: Rail freight handling

(a) Scope. This class comprises the providing of rail freight handling as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. (d) Scheduled rate. The scheduled rate for this class is 0.3900 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. The export receipts of a line assessed under this class are deducted in computing its assessable receipts under section 2-005.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band III.

(g) Records. The validity of a declaration about receipts of rail freight handling is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-014 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The levy collection office must keep a register entry of every decision about the classification of rail freight handling for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request in writing.

(d) Scheduled rate. For the purposes of section 2-010 the scheduled rate of this class is 0.6100 per cent.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band III.

(g) Records. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Commissioner may give the Registrar of Filers a register entry concerning the classification of agricultural contracting and harvesting, and the register entry must be given in the approved electronic form within sixty days after the relevant event.

### Section 2-152. Class AC-152: Horticultural nurseries

(a) Scope. This class comprises the carrying on of horticultural nurseries as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate.
