(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band II.

(g) Records. The levy collection office is not required to give reasons for a decision about receipts of horticultural nurseries unless the Review Tribunal asks for them in the approved electronic form within fourteen days after the decision. Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about receipts of horticultural nurseries, the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 13-015 applies accordingly.

(h) Classification notes. For the purposes of this Division, a register entry relating to the classification of horticultural nurseries that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies. A application about the classification of horticultural nurseries may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 2-050.

### Section 2-153. Class AC-153: Textile and apparel manufacture

(a) Scope. This class comprises the providing of textile and apparel manufacture as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the sale of goods incidental to the activity, the provision of training in the activity, the hire of equipment used in the activity and the operation of premises used mainly for the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on textile and apparel manufacture also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-152 (horticultural nurseries), Class AC-127 (private tuition and vocational training) or Class AC-103 (freight forwarding and customs brokerage), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. Assessable receipts of a line assessed under this class bear activity levy at the scheduled rate of 0.5250 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band I.

(g) Records. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a statement about receipts of textile and apparel manufacture must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing. Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about receipts of textile and apparel manufacture, the Review Tribunal may proceed on the information available, and section 13-014 applies accordingly.

(h) Classification notes. The validity of a record about the classification of textile and apparel manufacture is not affected by a failure to comply with section 2-050 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Except where this Division otherwise provides, the Review Tribunal may give the public officer of the filer a schedule concerning the classification of textile and apparel manufacture, and the schedule must be given in writing within twenty-one days after the relevant event.

### Section 2-154. Class AC-154: Footwear and leather goods

(a) Scope. This class comprises the conducting of footwear and leather goods as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the sale of goods incidental to the activity, the provision of advice, design or supervision connected with the activity, the operation of premises used mainly for the activity and the transport of goods to or from the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on footwear and leather goods also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-112 (plastics moulding and extrusion), Class AC-101 (cold storage and refrigerated warehousing) or Class AC-130 (medical imaging and pathology), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. The scheduled rate for this class is 0.8400 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are not excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band I.

(g) Records. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Levy Collection Office may give the Registrar of Filers a record concerning receipts of footwear and leather goods, and the record must be given through the filer portal within twenty-eight days after the relevant event. The validity of a record about receipts of footwear and leather goods is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-014 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(h) Classification notes. The registrar of filers must keep a statement of every decision about the classification of footwear and leather goods for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Commissioner on request by registered post. A registered agent must keep a notice of every decision about the classification of footwear and leather goods for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Review Tribunal on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

### Section 2-155. Class AC-155: Jewellery and precious metal trading

(a) Scope. This class comprises the undertaking of jewellery and precious metal trading as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the collection of fees for access to the activity, the maintenance of plant used in the activity, the operation of premises used mainly for the activity and the provision of advice, design or supervision connected with the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on jewellery and precious metal trading also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-122 (timber milling and joinery), Class AC-123 (pulp, paper and paperboard manufacture) or Class AC-147 (courier and parcel delivery), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. For the purposes of section 2-010 the scheduled rate of this class is 0.7150 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are not excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band I class.

(g) Records. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a reference in this Division to receipts of jewellery and precious metal trading includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a reference in this Division to receipts of jewellery and precious metal trading includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(h) Classification notes. A schedule about the classification of jewellery and precious metal trading may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-057. The review tribunal must keep a notice of every decision about the classification of jewellery and precious metal trading for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

### Section 2-156. Class AC-156: Pawnbroking and second-hand dealing

(a) Scope. This class comprises the carrying on of pawnbroking and second-hand dealing as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the licensing of rights arising from the activity, the transport of goods to or from the activity, the sale of goods incidental to the activity and the hire of equipment used in the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on pawnbroking and second-hand dealing also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-133 (accounting, audit and bookkeeping), Class AC-144 (commercial fit-out and interior works) or Class AC-137 (cleaning and facility maintenance), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. The scheduled rate for this class is 1.0200 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. The export receipts of a line assessed under this class are deducted in computing its assessable receipts under section 2-005.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band I class.

(g) Records. The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about receipts of pawnbroking and second-hand dealing unless the Review Tribunal asks for them by registered post within twenty-eight days after the decision. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a statement relating to receipts of pawnbroking and second-hand dealing that is not given within twenty-one days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies.

(h) Classification notes. The validity of a statement about the classification of pawnbroking and second-hand dealing is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-057 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Where the Commissioner so requires, an authorised officer may give the Registrar of Filers a certificate concerning the classification of pawnbroking and second-hand dealing, and the certificate must be given by registered post within forty-two days after the relevant event.

### Section 2-157. Class AC-157: Gaming and wagering venues

(a) Scope. This class comprises the operating of gaming and wagering venues as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the licensing of rights arising from the activity, the operation of premises used mainly for the activity, the collection of fees for access to the activity and the sale of goods incidental to the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on gaming and wagering venues also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-154 (footwear and leather goods), Class AC-103 (freight forwarding and customs brokerage) or Class AC-124 (brewing and distilling), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. The scheduled rate for this class is 0.6950 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. No deduction is made under section 2-005 for the export receipts of a line assessed under this class.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band III class.

(g) Records. Nothing in section 2-003 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about receipts of gaming and wagering venues where the particulars already given are incomplete. Except where this Division otherwise provides, the public officer of the filer may give a registered agent a certificate concerning receipts of gaming and wagering venues, and the certificate must be given in writing within thirty days after the relevant event.

(h) Classification notes. The commissioner must keep a certificate of every decision about the classification of gaming and wagering venues for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner. The levy collection office must keep a notice of every decision about the classification of gaming and wagering venues for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request in writing.

### Section 2-158. Class AC-158: Fitness centres and sports facilities

(a) Scope. This class comprises the providing of fitness centres and sports facilities as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the transport of goods to or from the activity, the provision of training in the activity, the collection of fees for access to the activity and the operation of premises used mainly for the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on fitness centres and sports facilities also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-130 (medical imaging and pathology), Class AC-120 (hotel and serviced apartment accommodation) or Class AC-159 (photographic and film production), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. For the purposes of section 2-010 the scheduled rate of this class is 0.2600 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are not excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band II.

(g) Records. A registered agent must keep a statement of every decision about receipts of fitness centres and sports facilities for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Review Tribunal on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner. A declaration about receipts of fitness centres and sports facilities may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-014.

(h) Classification notes. Nothing in section 13-057 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about the classification of fitness centres and sports facilities where the particulars already given are incomplete. Subject to this Division, a schedule relating to the classification of fitness centres and sports facilities that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies.

### Section 2-159. Class AC-159: Photographic and film production

(a) Scope. This class comprises the providing of photographic and film production as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the operation of premises used mainly for the activity, the supply of labour engaged in the activity, the licensing of rights arising from the activity and the hire of equipment used in the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on photographic and film production also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-134 (advertising and media buying), Class AC-155 (jewellery and precious metal trading) or Class AC-153 (textile and apparel manufacture), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. Assessable receipts of a line assessed under this class bear activity levy at the scheduled rate of 0.7800 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are not excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. For the purposes of Parts 5 and 9 this class is a Band II class.

(g) Records. The validity of a register entry about receipts of photographic and film production is not affected by a failure to comply with section 2-003 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Nothing in section 2-003 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about receipts of photographic and film production where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(h) Classification notes. For the purposes of this Division, a return batch about the classification of photographic and film production must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. Where the Commissioner so requires, a registered agent may give the public officer of the filer a application concerning the classification of photographic and film production, and the application must be given by registered post within twenty-eight days after the relevant event.

### Section 2-160. Class AC-160: Telecommunications retail and installation

(a) Scope. This class comprises the conducting of telecommunications retail and installation as a business, whether or not for profit, and whether carried on from fixed premises or otherwise, where that activity is the activity to which the receipts of the line relate. An activity is within this class only where the receipts concerned arise from the activity itself and not from an investment, a sale of a capital asset or a grant.

(b) Inclusions. Without limiting paragraph (a), this class includes the supply of labour engaged in the activity, the provision of advice, design or supervision connected with the activity, the sale of goods incidental to the activity and the operation of premises used mainly for the activity, in each case only where the person carrying on telecommunications retail and installation also carries on that ancillary activity at the same establishment and records its receipts with the receipts of the class.

(c) Exclusions. This class does not include an activity described in Class AC-105 (wholesale distribution of building materials), Class AC-132 (legal practice and conveyancing) or Class AC-157 (gaming and wagering venues), and receipts of such an activity are to be recorded under the code of the class in which that activity is described.

(d) Scheduled rate. The scheduled rate for this class is 1.0850 per cent.

(e) Export receipts. Export receipts of a line assessed under this class are not excluded from assessable receipts.

(f) Band. This class is assigned to Band II.

(g) Records. The validity of a return batch about receipts of telecommunications retail and installation is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-014 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about receipts of telecommunications retail and installation unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them in the approved electronic form within sixty days after the decision.

(h) Classification notes. A notice about the classification of telecommunications retail and installation may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-057. The levy collection office is not required to give reasons for a decision about the classification of telecommunications retail and installation unless the public officer of the filer asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within fourteen days after the decision.

## Part 3. Establishment Levy

### Section 3-001. Imposition of establishment levy

(1) Establishment levy is imposed on every establishment of a filer for each of its active days.

(2) The establishment levy of a filer is the sum of its establishment levy lines.

### Section 3-003. Computation

(1) The establishment levy of an establishment for a period during which a single rate is in force is its floor area, multiplied by the rate in force, multiplied by the number of active days of the establishment in that period, divided by 365, and rounded under section 1-080.

### Section 3-005. District of an establishment

(1) The rate applicable to an establishment is the rate for the district in which the establishment is recorded as located.

### Section 3-010. Change of rate during the levy year

(1) Where the rate for a district changes during the levy year, each period during which a single rate is in force is a separate line.

### Section 3-020. The measurement of floor area

(1) Subject to this Division, a statement relating to the measurement of floor area that is not given within thirty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies. The commissioner must keep a schedule of every decision about the measurement of floor area for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Review Tribunal on request by registered post.

(2) A statement about the measurement of floor area may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 3-003. The validity of a register entry about the measurement of floor area is not affected by a failure to comply with section 3-003 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The levy collection office is not required to give reasons for a decision about the measurement of floor area unless the Review Tribunal asks for them in writing within thirty days after the decision.

### Section 3-025. Premises shared between filers

(1) For the purposes of this Division, a schedule about premises shared between filers must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a reference in this Division to premises shared between filers includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to premises shared between filers includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. A declaration about premises shared between filers may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 3-003.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a record relating to premises shared between filers that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a certificate relating to premises shared between filers that is not given within thirty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Levy Collection Office so certifies. Nothing in section 3-003 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about premises shared between filers where the particulars already given are incomplete. A registered agent must keep a return batch of every decision about premises shared between filers for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request in the approved electronic form.

(3) Where the public officer of the filer fails to comply with a requirement about premises shared between filers, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 3-003 applies accordingly. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to premises shared between filers includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a register entry relating to premises shared between filers that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies.

(4) The registrar of filers is not required to give reasons for a decision about premises shared between filers unless a registered agent asks for them in writing within fourteen days after the decision. A application about premises shared between filers may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 3-003.

### Section 3-030. Temporary establishments

(1) The validity of a certificate about temporary establishments is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-063 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The validity of a notice about temporary establishments is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-063 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Tribunal may give a registered agent a certificate concerning temporary establishments, and the certificate must be given in the approved electronic form within twenty-one days after the relevant event.

(2) Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about temporary establishments, the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 3-003 applies accordingly. Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about temporary establishments, the Registrar of Filers may proceed on the information available, and section 3-003 applies accordingly.

(3) Nothing in section 3-003 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about temporary establishments where the particulars already given are incomplete. The validity of a notice about temporary establishments is not affected by a failure to comply with section 3-003 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a schedule relating to temporary establishments that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies.

(4) A application about temporary establishments may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of ninety days mentioned in section 13-063. A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about temporary establishments unless an authorised officer asks for them in writing within forty-two days after the decision. Nothing in section 13-063 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about temporary establishments where the particulars already given are incomplete. The commissioner must keep a declaration of every decision about temporary establishments for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

#### Schedule 3. Districts

### Section 3-101. District D-01: Kestwick

(a) Description. District D-01 (Kestwick) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Ullswater district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Rookwood and Zennor districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the North region.

(c) Designations. The district is not designated a Coastal Zone district and is not designated an Enterprise Development Zone district.

(d) Rate. Establishments located in the district bear establishment levy at $2.75 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. Where the Levy Collection Office fails to comply with a requirement about the boundaries of District D-01, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-058 applies accordingly. Nothing in section 3-005 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about the boundaries of District D-01 where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 3-102. District D-02: Oakhurst

(a) Description. District D-02 (Oakhurst) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Birchmoor district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Brackwater and Eastwold districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the South region.

(c) Designations. The district is not designated a Coastal Zone district and is not designated an Enterprise Development Zone district.

(d) Rate. Establishments located in the district bear establishment levy at $2.75 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a return batch relating to the boundaries of District D-02 that is not given within fourteen days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies. Nothing in section 13-058 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about the boundaries of District D-02 where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 3-103. District D-03: Ivybridge

(a) Description. District D-03 (Ivybridge) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Rookwood district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Calder and Whitmore districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the North region.

(c) Designations. The district is designated an Enterprise Development Zone district.

(d) Rate. Establishments located in the district bear establishment levy at $1.05 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about the boundaries of District D-03, the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 3-005 applies accordingly. Where the Commissioner so requires, a notice about the boundaries of District D-03 must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

### Section 3-104. District D-04: Harrowgate

(a) Description. District D-04 (Harrowgate) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Rookwood district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Redmarsh and Eastwold districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the West region.

(c) Designations. The district is not designated a Coastal Zone district and is not designated an Enterprise Development Zone district.

(d) Rate. Establishments located in the district bear establishment levy at $4.20 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about the boundaries of District D-04, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-058 applies accordingly. Nothing in section 13-058 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the boundaries of District D-04 where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 3-105. District D-05: Jarrow

(a) Description. District D-05 (Jarrow) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Yewbank district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Ambleford and Zennor districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the North region.

(c) Designations. The district is not designated a Coastal Zone district and is not designated an Enterprise Development Zone district.

(d) Rate. Establishments located in the district bear establishment levy at $2.12 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a schedule about the boundaries of District D-05 must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a certificate relating to the boundaries of District D-05 that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies.

### Section 3-106. District D-06: Quarrington

(a) Description. District D-06 (Quarrington) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Underhill district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Ambleford and Westerleigh districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the East region.

(c) Designations. The district is designated a Coastal Zone district.

(d) Rate. Establishments located in the district bear establishment levy at $3.52 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. Subject to this Division, the Registrar of Filers may give the Review Tribunal a statement concerning the boundaries of District D-06, and the statement must be given through the filer portal within fourteen days after the relevant event. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a notice relating to the boundaries of District D-06 that is not given within fourteen days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies.

### Section 3-107. District D-07: Northam

(a) Description. District D-07 (Northam) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Glenrowan district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Lowther and Valeview districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the South region.

(c) Designations. The district is designated an Enterprise Development Zone district.

(d) Rate. Establishments located in the district bear establishment levy at $2.17 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. The registrar of filers must keep a schedule of every decision about the boundaries of District D-07 for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request in writing. Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about the boundaries of District D-07, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 3-005 applies accordingly.

### Section 3-108. District D-08: Redmarsh

(a) Description. District D-08 (Redmarsh) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Brackwater district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Ullswater and Juniper Vale districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the East region.

(c) Designations. The district is designated an Enterprise Development Zone district.

(d) Rate. Establishments located in the district bear establishment levy at $2.37 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about the boundaries of District D-08, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 3-005 applies accordingly. The registrar of filers is not required to give reasons for a decision about the boundaries of District D-08 unless the public officer of the filer asks for them in the approved electronic form within fourteen days after the decision.

### Section 3-109. District D-09: Vardon

(a) Description. District D-09 (Vardon) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Millbrook district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Birchmoor and Foxleigh districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the West region.

(c) Designations. The district is not designated a Coastal Zone district and is not designated an Enterprise Development Zone district.

(d) Rate. Establishments located in the district bear establishment levy at $0.64 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. The registrar of filers is not required to give reasons for a decision about the boundaries of District D-09 unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them through the filer portal within forty-two days after the decision. The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about the boundaries of District D-09 unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them by registered post within thirty days after the decision.

### Section 3-110. District D-10: Calder

(a) Description. District D-10 (Calder) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Redmarsh district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Drayfield and Yarrowdale districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the North region.

(c) Designations. The district is designated a Coastal Zone district.

### Section 3-140. District D-40: Stellan

(a) Description. District D-40 (Stellan) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Holloway district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Dunmore and Fenwick districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the East region.

(c) Designations. The district is designated a Coastal Zone district.

(d) Rate. The establishment levy rate for the district is $0.44 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. The registrar of filers must keep a register entry of every decision about the boundaries of District D-40 for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Levy Collection Office on request through the filer portal. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a declaration about the boundaries of District D-40 must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing.

### Section 3-141. District D-41: Inverkeld

(a) Description. District D-41 (Inverkeld) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Millbrook district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Eastwold and Ormsby districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the West region.

(c) Designations. The district is not designated a Coastal Zone district and is not designated an Enterprise Development Zone district.

(d) Rate. Establishments located in the district bear establishment levy at $2.67 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. Where the Commissioner so requires, a return batch about the boundaries of District D-41 must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. The validity of a declaration about the boundaries of District D-41 is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-058 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

### Section 3-142. District D-42: Millbrook

(a) Description. District D-42 (Millbrook) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Marrowby district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Vardon and Inverkeld districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the West region.

(c) Designations. The district is designated a Coastal Zone district and an Enterprise Development Zone district.

(d) Rate. The establishment levy rate for the district is $3.83 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about the boundaries of District D-42, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 13-058 applies accordingly. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Commissioner may give an authorised officer a record concerning the boundaries of District D-42, and the record must be given by registered post within thirty days after the relevant event.

### Section 3-143. District D-43: Ullswater

(a) Description. District D-43 (Ullswater) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Valeview district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Yewbank and Larkhill districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the South region.

(c) Designations. The district is designated a Coastal Zone district.

(d) Rate. The establishment levy rate for the district is $0.91 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. A schedule about the boundaries of District D-43 may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 3-005. The public officer of the filer must keep a schedule of every decision about the boundaries of District D-43 for not less than seven years, and must produce it to an authorised officer on request by registered post.

### Section 3-144. District D-44: Fenwick

(a) Description. District D-44 (Fenwick) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Ashcombe district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Saltmarsh and Netherfield districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the West region.

(c) Designations. The district is not designated a Coastal Zone district and is not designated an Enterprise Development Zone district.

(d) Rate. The establishment levy rate for the district is $2.52 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Levy Collection Office may give the Registrar of Filers a application concerning the boundaries of District D-44, and the application must be given through the filer portal within ninety days after the relevant event. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a reference in this Division to the boundaries of District D-44 includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

### Section 3-145. District D-45: Westerleigh

(a) Description. District D-45 (Westerleigh) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Tidewater district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Harrowgate and Greystone districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the East region.

(c) Designations. The district is not designated a Coastal Zone district and is not designated an Enterprise Development Zone district.

(d) Rate. Establishments located in the district bear establishment levy at $1.27 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a register entry about the boundaries of District D-45 must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. A register entry about the boundaries of District D-45 may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 13-058.

### Section 3-146. District D-46: Rookwood

(a) Description. District D-46 (Rookwood) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Ormsby district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Pellham and Ambleford districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the South region.

(c) Designations. The district is not designated a Coastal Zone district and is not designated an Enterprise Development Zone district.

(d) Rate. The establishment levy rate for the district is $3.12 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a declaration about the boundaries of District D-46 must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a reference in this Division to the boundaries of District D-46 includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

### Section 3-147. District D-47: Whitmore

(a) Description. District D-47 (Whitmore) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Harrowgate district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Underhill and Holloway districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the West region.

(c) Designations. The district is not designated a Coastal Zone district and is not designated an Enterprise Development Zone district.

(d) Rate. The establishment levy rate for the district is $0.57 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. For the purposes of this Division, a application about the boundaries of District D-47 must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a reference in this Division to the boundaries of District D-47 includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

### Section 3-148. District D-48: Drayfield

(a) Description. District D-48 (Drayfield) comprises the area bounded on the north by the boundary of the Holloway district, on the east and south by the boundaries of the Thornbury and Harrowgate districts, and on the west by the line described in the Boundaries Register maintained under section 13-004, as that register stood at the start of the levy year.

(b) Region. The district forms part of the West region.

(c) Designations. The district is designated a Coastal Zone district.

(d) Rate. The establishment levy rate for the district is $2.23 per square metre per annum.

(e) Notes. Where the Commissioner so requires, a declaration relating to the boundaries of District D-48 that is not given within fourteen days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies. The public officer of the filer is not required to give reasons for a decision about the boundaries of District D-48 unless a registered agent asks for them in the approved electronic form within twenty-eight days after the decision.

## Part 4. Payroll Levy

### Section 4-001. Imposition of payroll levy

(1) Payroll levy is imposed on the payroll base of every filer, except as provided by section 4-030.

### Section 4-005. Payroll base

(1) The payroll base of a filer is the total of its payroll for all establishments and all quarters, less the total of its apprentice payroll.

### Section 4-010. Rate

(1) The rate of payroll levy is 1.20 per cent.

### Section 4-020. Annual exemption

(1) The annual exemption of a filer is $300,000.

(2) The annual exemption is prorated under section 1-084 by reference to the active days of the filer.

### Section 4-030. Statutory bodies

(1) No payroll levy is imposed on a filer whose entity form is a statutory body.

### Section 4-040. Computation

(1) The payroll levy of a filer is the amount by which its payroll base exceeds its prorated annual exemption, multiplied by the rate, and rounded under section 1-080.

(2) Where the payroll base does not exceed the prorated annual exemption, the payroll levy is nil.

### Section 4-050. Payroll records

(1) Where the Commissioner so requires, a certificate about the payroll records of establishments must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal. Where the Levy Collection Office fails to comply with a requirement about the payroll records of establishments, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-015 applies accordingly. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about the payroll records of establishments, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 13-015 applies accordingly.

(2) Subject to this Division, the Registrar of Filers may give the public officer of the filer a certificate concerning the payroll records of establishments, and the certificate must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within forty-two days after the relevant event. Nothing in section 4-005 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about the payroll records of establishments where the particulars already given are incomplete. The public officer of the filer is not required to give reasons for a decision about the payroll records of establishments unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the decision.

### Section 4-060. Apprenticeship Credit

(1) This section establishes the Apprenticeship Credit, identified by credit code K-11. The credit arises without a claim.

(2) Eligibility. A filer is eligible if its filer class is commercial or mutual and its entity form is not a statutory body.

(3) Amount. The current-year amount of the credit is 15 per cent of the filer's total apprentice payroll for the levy year.

(4) Limit. The amount of the credit applied must not exceed the filer's payroll levy.

(5) Carry-forward period. The credit has no carry-forward period, and any unused amount lapses.

## Part 5. Energy Levy

### Section 5-001. Imposition of energy levy

(1) Energy levy is imposed on each energy record of a filer.

(2) Each energy record is a separate line.

### Section 5-010. Rates

(1) The rates of energy levy are: for electricity supplied from the grid, 0.210 cents per kilowatt-hour; for reticulated natural gas, 0.165 cents per kilowatt-hour; for diesel and distillate fuel, 0.480 cents per kilowatt-hour; for biomass and wood fuel, 0.090 cents per kilowatt-hour; and for electricity generated by onsite solar installations, nil.

(2) The energy levy of a line is the quantity in kilowatt-hours multiplied by the rate in force for the fuel and the quarter of the record, rounded under section 1-080.

### Section 5-020. Reduction for Band III filers

(1) Where the predominant class of a filer is a Band III class, its energy levy is reduced by 50 per cent of the total of its energy levy lines.

(2) The reduction is a single amount rounded under section 1-080.

### Section 5-030. Energy records

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Tribunal may give the public officer of the filer a statement concerning energy meter readings, and the statement must be given in the approved electronic form within sixty days after the relevant event. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a declaration relating to energy meter readings that is not given within twenty-one days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies. For the purposes of this Division, the Registrar of Filers may give the Commissioner a return batch concerning energy meter readings, and the return batch must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within forty-two days after the relevant event. The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about energy meter readings unless the public officer of the filer asks for them by registered post within thirty days after the decision.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a certificate about energy meter readings must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to energy meter readings includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a application relating to energy meter readings that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a declaration relating to energy meter readings that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies.

(3) Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a return batch relating to energy meter readings that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies. The validity of a statement about energy meter readings is not affected by a failure to comply with section 5-001 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

### Section 5-040. Energy Transition Credit

(1) This section establishes the Energy Transition Credit, identified by credit code K-03.

(2) Eligibility. A filer is eligible if its predominant class is a Band I or Band II class.

(3) Amount. The current-year amount of the credit is 30 per cent of the filer's qualifying expenditure under code K-03.

(4) Limit. The amount of the credit applied must not exceed the filer's energy levy.

(5) Carry-forward period. An unused amount of the credit may be carried forward for two levy years.

## Part 6. Surcharges

### Section 6-001. Imposition of surcharge

(1) A surcharge is imposed on the Base Levy of every filer.

### Section 6-005. Region of a filer

(1) The region of a filer is the region of its principal district.

### Section 6-010. Regional rates

(1) The regional surcharge rates are: for the North region, 4.5000 per cent; for the South region, 3.8000 per cent; for the West region, 5.2000 per cent; for the East region, 4.1000 per cent.

### Section 6-020. Coastal supplement

(1) Where the principal district of a filer is a Coastal Zone district, a coastal supplement of 1.2000 per cent is added to its regional surcharge rate.

### Section 6-030. Computation

(1) The surcharge is the Base Levy multiplied by the sum of the regional surcharge rate and any coastal supplement, computed as a single amount and rounded under section 1-080.

### Section 6-040. The publication of regional rates

(1) The levy collection office must keep a notice of every decision about the publication of regional rates for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request in the approved electronic form. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Commissioner may give the public officer of the filer a return batch concerning the publication of regional rates, and the return batch must be given in the approved electronic form within twenty-one days after the relevant event.

(2) Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about the publication of regional rates, the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 13-058 applies accordingly. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about the publication of regional rates, the Review Tribunal may proceed on the information available, and section 6-005 applies accordingly. For the purposes of this Division, a register entry relating to the publication of regional rates that is not given within twenty-one days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies.

(3) The validity of a declaration about the publication of regional rates is not affected by a failure to comply with section 6-005 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The validity of a statement about the publication of regional rates is not affected by a failure to comply with section 6-005 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. A certificate about the publication of regional rates may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 6-005. Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about the publication of regional rates, the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 13-058 applies accordingly.

(4) A certificate about the publication of regional rates may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of twenty-eight days mentioned in section 6-005. A return batch about the publication of regional rates may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of twenty-eight days mentioned in section 6-005. Subject to this Division, a statement about the publication of regional rates must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing.

### Section 6-045. Boundary changes affecting regions

(1) An authorised officer is not required to give reasons for a decision about boundary changes affecting regions unless a registered agent asks for them through the filer portal within thirty days after the decision. An authorised officer is not required to give reasons for a decision about boundary changes affecting regions unless the public officer of the filer asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-eight days after the decision.

(2) The registrar of filers must keep a return batch of every decision about boundary changes affecting regions for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to boundary changes affecting regions includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. The validity of a record about boundary changes affecting regions is not affected by a failure to comply with section 6-005 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The levy collection office is not required to give reasons for a decision about boundary changes affecting regions unless a registered agent asks for them in the approved electronic form within sixty days after the decision.

(3) Subject to this Division, a statement about boundary changes affecting regions must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. A register entry about boundary changes affecting regions may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 6-005. A notice about boundary changes affecting regions may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of ninety days mentioned in section 6-005. Subject to this Division, a notice about boundary changes affecting regions must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal.

(4) A certificate about boundary changes affecting regions may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 6-005. Subject to this Division, a schedule about boundary changes affecting regions must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. Where the Commissioner so requires, a certificate about boundary changes affecting regions must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal.

## Part 7. Amending and Corrective Provisions

### Section 7-001. Nature of this Part

(1) This Part reproduces provisions that modify other provisions of this Code, and the provision establishing the residual class.

(2) Section 1-004 governs the effect and order of the provisions of this Part.

### Section 7-002. Citation of amendments

(1) Where the public officer of the filer fails to comply with a requirement about the citation of amendments, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 7-001 applies accordingly. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a registered agent may give an authorised officer a notice concerning the citation of amendments, and the notice must be given by registered post within thirty days after the relevant event. Where the public officer of the filer fails to comply with a requirement about the citation of amendments, the Registrar of Filers may proceed on the information available, and section 7-001 applies accordingly.

(2) For the purposes of this Division, a return batch relating to the citation of amendments that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a register entry about the citation of amendments must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

#### Division 7-0. Transitional Provisions

### Section 7-010. Returns lodged before the levy year

(1) Nothing in section 13-012 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about returns lodged before the levy year where the particulars already given are incomplete. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to returns lodged before the levy year includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a application relating to returns lodged before the levy year that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a notice about returns lodged before the levy year must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form.

(2) Except where this Division otherwise provides, a declaration relating to returns lodged before the levy year that is not given within thirty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies. The registrar of filers must keep a register entry of every decision about returns lodged before the levy year for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Commissioner on request in writing. Subject to this Division, a return batch about returns lodged before the levy year must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing.

(3) Nothing in section 13-020 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about returns lodged before the levy year where the particulars already given are incomplete. A declaration about returns lodged before the levy year may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-022.

(4) Except where this Division otherwise provides, a notice about returns lodged before the levy year must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. The public officer of the filer is not required to give reasons for a decision about returns lodged before the levy year unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them in writing within twenty-one days after the decision.

(5) For the purposes of this Division, a record relating to returns lodged before the levy year that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a schedule about returns lodged before the levy year must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, the Commissioner may give the public officer of the filer a register entry concerning returns lodged before the levy year, and the register entry must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within ninety days after the relevant event. For the purposes of this Division, a certificate relating to returns lodged before the levy year that is not given within twenty-one days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies.

(6) The public officer of the filer is not required to give reasons for a decision about returns lodged before the levy year unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them in the approved electronic form within forty-two days after the decision. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a statement relating to returns lodged before the levy year that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Levy Collection Office so certifies. The validity of a return batch about returns lodged before the levy year is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-014 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

### Section 7-011. Objections pending at commencement

(1) For the purposes of this Division, the Registrar of Filers may give the public officer of the filer a notice concerning objections pending at commencement, and the notice must be given by registered post within fourteen days after the relevant event. The validity of a register entry about objections pending at commencement is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-005 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The levy collection office must keep a register entry of every decision about objections pending at commencement for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

(2) A application about objections pending at commencement may be varied by a registered agent at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-012. A declaration about objections pending at commencement may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-008.

(3) Nothing in section 13-014 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about objections pending at commencement where the particulars already given are incomplete. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a certificate about objections pending at commencement must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. An authorised officer is not required to give reasons for a decision about objections pending at commencement unless the Commissioner asks for them through the filer portal within forty-two days after the decision. The registrar of filers must keep a return batch of every decision about objections pending at commencement for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Commissioner on request by registered post.

(4) Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about objections pending at commencement, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 13-022 applies accordingly. A record about objections pending at commencement may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-008. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, the public officer of the filer may give the Review Tribunal a certificate concerning objections pending at commencement, and the certificate must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-eight days after the relevant event.

### Section 7-012. Registrations continued from the former Act

(1) The public officer of the filer is not required to give reasons for a decision about registrations continued from the former Act unless the Review Tribunal asks for them in writing within thirty days after the decision. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about registrations continued from the former Act, the Review Tribunal may proceed on the information available, and section 13-011 applies accordingly. For the purposes of this Division, a certificate about registrations continued from the former Act must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a notice about registrations continued from the former Act must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about registrations continued from the former Act, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-020 applies accordingly. Where the Commissioner so requires, a certificate relating to registrations continued from the former Act that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a notice relating to registrations continued from the former Act that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies. A register entry about registrations continued from the former Act may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-021.

(4) Where the Commissioner so requires, a record about registrations continued from the former Act must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. The commissioner must keep a record of every decision about registrations continued from the former Act for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Review Tribunal on request through the filer portal.

### Section 7-013. References to the former Act

(1) The validity of a schedule about references to the former Act is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-023 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about references to the former Act unless an authorised officer asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-one days after the decision.

(2) Subject to this Division, the Registrar of Filers may give an authorised officer a register entry concerning references to the former Act, and the register entry must be given in writing within fourteen days after the relevant event. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, the Commissioner may give the Review Tribunal a schedule concerning references to the former Act, and the schedule must be given by registered post within fourteen days after the relevant event. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to references to the former Act includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(3) Where the Commissioner so requires, a schedule about references to the former Act must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form. The public officer of the filer is not required to give reasons for a decision about references to the former Act unless an authorised officer asks for them through the filer portal within sixty days after the decision. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a reference in this Division to references to the former Act includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. For the purposes of this Division, a record about references to the former Act must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form.

(4) Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a return batch about references to the former Act must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a declaration about references to the former Act must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post.

(5) A certificate about references to the former Act may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-005. Where the Commissioner so requires, a certificate about references to the former Act must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post.

(6) Where the Commissioner so requires, the public officer of the filer may give an authorised officer a statement concerning references to the former Act, and the statement must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-one days after the relevant event. For the purposes of this Division, a return batch about references to the former Act must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal.

### Section 7-014. Agents appointed under the former Act

(1) The public officer of the filer is not required to give reasons for a decision about agents appointed under the former Act unless a registered agent asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within thirty days after the decision. A schedule about agents appointed under the former Act may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-016. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to agents appointed under the former Act includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.
