(2) For the purposes of this Division, a return batch about records kept under the former Act (supplementary provision) must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. For the purposes of this Division, a register entry relating to records kept under the former Act (supplementary provision) that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies.

(3) Subject to this Division, a certificate relating to records kept under the former Act (supplementary provision) that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies. Except where this Division otherwise provides, an authorised officer may give the public officer of the filer a statement concerning records kept under the former Act (supplementary provision), and the statement must be given by registered post within twenty-eight days after the relevant event. The validity of a certificate about records kept under the former Act (supplementary provision) is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-013 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. A registered agent must keep a record of every decision about records kept under the former Act (supplementary provision) for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request through the filer portal.

(4) Where the Commissioner so requires, a statement relating to records kept under the former Act (supplementary provision) that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies. A certificate about records kept under the former Act (supplementary provision) may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-007. The registrar of filers must keep a declaration of every decision about records kept under the former Act (supplementary provision) for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request through the filer portal.

(5) The commissioner must keep a return batch of every decision about records kept under the former Act (supplementary provision) for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Registrar of Filers on request by registered post. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a register entry about records kept under the former Act (supplementary provision) must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. A certificate about records kept under the former Act (supplementary provision) may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of ninety days mentioned in section 13-011. The validity of a schedule about records kept under the former Act (supplementary provision) is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-017 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(6) For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to records kept under the former Act (supplementary provision) includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. The levy collection office must keep a register entry of every decision about records kept under the former Act (supplementary provision) for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request through the filer portal.

### Section 7-028. Penalties incurred under the former Act (supplementary provision)

(1) A certificate about penalties incurred under the former Act (supplementary provision) may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 13-007. The validity of a notice about penalties incurred under the former Act (supplementary provision) is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-007 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. A declaration about penalties incurred under the former Act (supplementary provision) may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of ninety days mentioned in section 13-012.

(2) Except where this Division otherwise provides, an authorised officer may give the Registrar of Filers a certificate concerning penalties incurred under the former Act (supplementary provision), and the certificate must be given in the approved electronic form within twenty-one days after the relevant event. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, the Review Tribunal may give the public officer of the filer a application concerning penalties incurred under the former Act (supplementary provision), and the application must be given by registered post within twenty-one days after the relevant event. Nothing in section 13-022 prevents an authorised officer from requiring further particulars about penalties incurred under the former Act (supplementary provision) where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(3) Nothing in section 13-022 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about penalties incurred under the former Act (supplementary provision) where the particulars already given are incomplete. The registrar of filers must keep a statement of every decision about penalties incurred under the former Act (supplementary provision) for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a notice relating to penalties incurred under the former Act (supplementary provision) that is not given within fourteen days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies. Subject to this Division, a registered agent may give the public officer of the filer a certificate concerning penalties incurred under the former Act (supplementary provision), and the certificate must be given in the approved electronic form within twenty-one days after the relevant event. Subject to this Division, the public officer of the filer may give the Registrar of Filers a return batch concerning penalties incurred under the former Act (supplementary provision), and the return batch must be given in writing within forty-two days after the relevant event.

### Section 7-029. Rulings issued under the former Act (supplementary provision)

(1) The review tribunal must keep a return batch of every decision about rulings issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request by registered post. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to rulings issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Nothing in section 13-014 prevents an authorised officer from requiring further particulars about rulings issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a record about rulings issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. Nothing in section 13-016 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about rulings issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(3) A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about rulings issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) unless the Commissioner asks for them in the approved electronic form within forty-two days after the decision. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a schedule relating to rulings issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) that is not given within fourteen days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies.

### Section 7-030. Forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision)

(1) The validity of a return batch about forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision) is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-011 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a declaration about forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision) must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post.

(2) The validity of a return batch about forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision) is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-012 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a declaration about forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision) must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. Nothing in section 13-020 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision) where the particulars already given are incomplete. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a register entry relating to forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision) that is not given within twenty-one days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies.

(3) Except where this Division otherwise provides, a register entry relating to forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision) that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies. Where the Commissioner so requires, a notice about forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision) must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing. The public officer of the filer must keep a schedule of every decision about forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision) for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Registrar of Filers on request by registered post.

(4) The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision) unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within forty-two days after the decision. A statement about forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision) may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-005. The registrar of filers must keep a register entry of every decision about forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision) for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Levy Collection Office on request in writing. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision) includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(5) The validity of a record about forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision) is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-016 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. A notice about forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision) may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-008. Where the Commissioner so requires, a registered agent may give the Commissioner a statement concerning forms approved under the former Act (supplementary provision), and the statement must be given through the filer portal within ninety days after the relevant event.

### Section 7-031. Delegations continued from the former Act (supplementary provision)

(1) The validity of a application about delegations continued from the former Act (supplementary provision) is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-006 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Where the Commissioner so requires, a application about delegations continued from the former Act (supplementary provision) must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a registered agent may give the Registrar of Filers a certificate concerning delegations continued from the former Act (supplementary provision), and the certificate must be given in writing within thirty days after the relevant event. The validity of a notice about delegations continued from the former Act (supplementary provision) is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-011 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(3) For the purposes of this Division, a notice about delegations continued from the former Act (supplementary provision) must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. A certificate about delegations continued from the former Act (supplementary provision) may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-018.

(4) Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about delegations continued from the former Act (supplementary provision), the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 13-004 applies accordingly. Where the Levy Collection Office fails to comply with a requirement about delegations continued from the former Act (supplementary provision), the Registrar of Filers may proceed on the information available, and section 13-005 applies accordingly. Subject to this Division, a application relating to delegations continued from the former Act (supplementary provision) that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies.

### Section 7-143. Coastal Resilience Credit

(1) This section establishes the Coastal Resilience Credit, identified by credit code K-12, as a credit of this Code.

(2) Eligibility. A filer is eligible for the credit if its principal district is a Coastal Zone district.

(3) Amount. The current-year amount of the credit is 25 per cent of the filer's qualifying expenditure under code K-12.

(4) Limit. The amount of the credit applied for the levy year must not exceed 10 per cent of the filer's Primary Levy.

(5) Carry-forward period. An unused amount of the credit may be carried forward for three levy years.

#### Division 7-3. Miscellaneous Provisions

### Section 7-301. Repeal

Subsection (5) of section 13-088 is repealed.

Any step taken under the repealed subsection before its repeal continues to have effect.

Application. For the purposes of this Division, a notice relating to steps taken under the provision amended that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies. Subject to this Division, a reference in this Division to steps taken under the provision amended includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a register entry about steps taken under the provision amended must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about steps taken under the provision amended unless the public officer of the filer asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-one days after the decision.

### Section 7-302. Manner of giving documents

In section 13-043, for 'in the approved electronic form' substitute 'in writing' wherever occurring.

Application. Where the public officer of the filer fails to comply with a requirement about steps taken under the provision amended, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-021 applies accordingly. Nothing in section 13-017 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about steps taken under the provision amended where the particulars already given are incomplete. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a certificate about steps taken under the provision amended must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form.

### Section 7-303. Repeal

Subsection (6) of section 13-062 is repealed.

Any step taken under the repealed subsection before its repeal continues to have effect.

Application. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a statement relating to steps taken under the provision amended that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a declaration relating to steps taken under the provision amended that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies. The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about steps taken under the provision amended unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them in the approved electronic form within fourteen days after the decision.

### Section 7-304. Exclusions of a class

In section 2-121(c), after 'Class AC-137' insert '(other than the activity described in section 13-054)'.

The amendment affects only the classification of activities and does not alter any rate.

Application. Where the Commissioner fails to comply with a requirement about steps taken under the provision amended, the Review Tribunal may proceed on the information available, and section 13-027 applies accordingly. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a registered agent may give the public officer of the filer a notice concerning steps taken under the provision amended, and the notice must be given in writing within fourteen days after the relevant event.

### Section 7-305. Scope of a class

In section 2-135(b), after 'includes' insert 'the hire of equipment used in the activity, '.

The amendment does not alter the scheduled rate, the treatment of export receipts or the band of the class.

Application. Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about steps taken under the provision amended, the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 13-016 applies accordingly. Nothing in section 13-005 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about steps taken under the provision amended where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 7-306. Repeal

Subsection (5) of section 13-023 is repealed.

Any step taken under the repealed subsection before its repeal continues to have effect.

Application. The public officer of the filer must keep a declaration of every decision about steps taken under the provision amended for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Levy Collection Office on request in writing. An authorised officer is not required to give reasons for a decision about steps taken under the provision amended unless the public officer of the filer asks for them in writing within forty-two days after the decision. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to steps taken under the provision amended includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

### Section 7-307. Penalty units

In section 13-027(1), for '30 penalty units' substitute '64 penalty units'.

Application. The registrar of filers must keep a record of every decision about steps taken under the provision amended for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Review Tribunal on request by registered post. Subject to this Division, a return batch about steps taken under the provision amended must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form.

### Section 7-308. Residual class

(1) This section establishes the residual class, Class AC-199 (unscheduled activities).

(2) A line whose activity code is not the code of a class described in Schedule 2 is assessed under Class AC-199.

(3) The scheduled rate of Class AC-199 is 0.5500 per cent.

(4) Export receipts of a line assessed under Class AC-199 are not excluded from assessable receipts.

(5) Class AC-199 is assigned to Band II.

(6) The Commissioner must record, for each line assessed under this section, that the line was reported under an unscheduled activity code.

### Section 7-309. Exclusions of a class

In section 2-112(c), after 'Class AC-141' insert '(other than the activity described in section 13-014)'.

The amendment affects only the classification of activities and does not alter any rate.

Application. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, the Registrar of Filers may give the Commissioner a declaration concerning steps taken under the provision amended, and the declaration must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within forty-two days after the relevant event. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a declaration relating to steps taken under the provision amended that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies.

### Section 7-310. Editorial amendment

In section 1-274 (definition of 'Registrar of Filers'), omit the words 'as recorded' where they last occur and insert 'as entered'.

The amendment is of an editorial nature and does not change the meaning of the definition.

Application. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a record relating to steps taken under the provision amended that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies. The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about steps taken under the provision amended unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within ninety days after the decision. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a record about steps taken under the provision amended must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post.

### Section 7-311. Receipts of inactive establishments

(1) A receipts record of an establishment for a quarter in which the establishment has no active day is assessed in the same way as any other receipts record.

(2) The Commissioner must record that receipts were reported for an inactive establishment in that quarter.

### Section 7-312. Time limit

In section 13-049(3), for 'twenty-eight days' substitute 'fourteen days'.

The substitution applies to steps taken on or after 1 March 2026.

Application. Subject to this Division, a statement about steps taken under the provision amended must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. A record about steps taken under the provision amended may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of twenty-eight days mentioned in section 13-026. Where the Commissioner so requires, a record about steps taken under the provision amended must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal.

#### Division 7-4. Consequential Provisions

### Section 7-401. Certificates issued under the former Act (supplementary provision)

(1) The validity of a certificate about certificates issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-015 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. (2) For the purposes of this Division, a declaration relating to certificates issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies. Subject to this Division, a statement about certificates issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing. The validity of a application about certificates issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-021 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(3) Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about certificates issued under the former Act (supplementary provision), the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-018 applies accordingly. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a reference in this Division to certificates issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about certificates issued under the former Act (supplementary provision), an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-008 applies accordingly.

(4) A registered agent must keep a statement of every decision about certificates issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Commissioner on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a register entry about certificates issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a application relating to certificates issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies. Nothing in section 13-004 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about certificates issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(5) Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to certificates issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about certificates issued under the former Act (supplementary provision) unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them through the filer portal within twenty-one days after the decision.

### Section 7-402. Proceedings pending at commencement (supplementary provision)

(1) Except where this Division otherwise provides, a reference in this Division to proceedings pending at commencement (supplementary provision) includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.
