Subject to this Division, a schedule relating to agents appointed under the former Act (further supplementary provision) that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies.

(4) A application about agents appointed under the former Act (further supplementary provision) may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of ninety days mentioned in section 13-021. Subject to this Division, the public officer of the filer may give the Review Tribunal a statement concerning agents appointed under the former Act (further supplementary provision), and the statement must be given in the approved electronic form within forty-two days after the relevant event. Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about agents appointed under the former Act (further supplementary provision), an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-016 applies accordingly. Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about agents appointed under the former Act (further supplementary provision), an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-009 applies accordingly.

### Section 7-408. Records kept under the former Act (further supplementary provision)

(1) The levy collection office must keep a application of every decision about records kept under the former Act (further supplementary provision) for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Registrar of Filers on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner. Nothing in section 13-011 prevents an authorised officer from requiring further particulars about records kept under the former Act (further supplementary provision) where the particulars already given are incomplete. An authorised officer is not required to give reasons for a decision about records kept under the former Act (further supplementary provision) unless the public officer of the filer asks for them by registered post within sixty days after the decision.

(2) The validity of a schedule about records kept under the former Act (further supplementary provision) is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-008 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Where the Commissioner fails to comply with a requirement about records kept under the former Act (further supplementary provision), an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-006 applies accordingly.

(3) Nothing in section 13-015 prevents an authorised officer from requiring further particulars about records kept under the former Act (further supplementary provision) where the particulars already given are incomplete. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a statement relating to records kept under the former Act (further supplementary provision) that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies.

### Section 7-409. Penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision)

(1) The review tribunal must keep a application of every decision about penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision) for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner. An authorised officer is not required to give reasons for a decision about penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision) unless the public officer of the filer asks for them in the approved electronic form within forty-two days after the decision. The validity of a declaration about penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision) is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-019 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(2) The review tribunal must keep a schedule of every decision about penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision) for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner. The validity of a certificate about penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision) is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-004 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(3) The validity of a application about penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision) is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-004 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Where the Commissioner so requires, a application about penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision) must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form. An authorised officer must keep a application of every decision about penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision) for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Commissioner on request through the filer portal. The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision) unless the public officer of the filer asks for them through the filer portal within ninety days after the decision.

(4) Where the Commissioner so requires, the Registrar of Filers may give a registered agent a notice concerning penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision), and the notice must be given through the filer portal within twenty-one days after the relevant event. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision) includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(5) Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a register entry about penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision) must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal. A certificate about penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision) may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of ninety days mentioned in section 13-019. Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision), the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 13-012 applies accordingly. Nothing in section 13-022 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about penalties incurred under the former Act (further supplementary provision) where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 7-410. Rulings issued under the former Act (further supplementary provision)

(1) Subject to this Division, a schedule relating to rulings issued under the former Act (further supplementary provision) that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies. A register entry about rulings issued under the former Act (further supplementary provision) may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of twenty-eight days mentioned in section 13-013. A return batch about rulings issued under the former Act (further supplementary provision) may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-013. Nothing in section 13-021 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about rulings issued under the former Act (further supplementary provision) where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(2) Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to rulings issued under the former Act (further supplementary provision) includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. A declaration about rulings issued under the former Act (further supplementary provision) may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-012. Subject to this Division, a reference in this Division to rulings issued under the former Act (further supplementary provision) includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Nothing in section 13-021 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about rulings issued under the former Act (further supplementary provision) where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(3) Nothing in section 13-020 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about rulings issued under the former Act (further supplementary provision) where the particulars already given are incomplete. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a schedule about rulings issued under the former Act (further supplementary provision) must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing.

#### Division 7-9. Consultation Draft: Proposed Amendments (not enacted)

### Section 7-900. Status of this Division

(1) This Division reproduces the Consultation Draft circulated before the enactment of Amending Act No. 3 of 2025. The proposals in it were not enacted. Section 1-004(6) applies.

### Section 7-901. Proposed establishment levy rate

It is proposed that the establishment levy rate for District D-12 be $2.10 per square metre per annum.

### Section 7-902. Proposed payroll exemption

It is proposed that, in section 4-020, for '$300,000' there be substituted '$350,000'.

### Section 7-903. Proposed scheduled rate

It is proposed that, with effect from 1 July 2026, the scheduled rate for Class AC-144 be 1.2150 per cent.

### Section 7-904. Proposed global limit

It is proposed that, in section 9-001, for '75 per cent' there be substituted '80 per cent'.

### Section 7-905. Proposed coastal supplement

It is proposed that the coastal supplement under section 6-020 be 2.0000 per cent.

### Section 7-906. Proposed scheduled rate

It is proposed that, with effect from 1 July 2026, the scheduled rate for Class AC-125 be 1.3850 per cent.

### Section 7-907. Proposed establishment levy rate

It is proposed that the establishment levy rate for District D-23 be $4.13 per square metre per annum.

### Section 7-908. Proposed scheduled rate

It is proposed that, with effect from 1 July 2026, the scheduled rate for Class AC-118 be 0.6700 per cent.

### Section 7-909. Proposed establishment levy rate

It is proposed that the establishment levy rate for District D-26 be $2.58 per square metre per annum.

### Section 7-910. Proposed scheduled rate

It is proposed that, with effect from 1 July 2026, the scheduled rate for Class AC-116 be 1.1400 per cent.

### Section 7-911. Proposed threshold

It is proposed that, in section 2-040(1)(b), for '$250,000' there be substituted '$500,000'.

### Section 7-912. Proposed Digital Infrastructure Credit

It is proposed to establish a Digital Infrastructure Credit, identified by credit code K-13, equal to 30 per cent of qualifying expenditure, limited to 20 per cent of Gross Levy, with no carry-forward period.

### Section 7-913. Proposed establishment levy rate

It is proposed that the establishment levy rate for District D-38 be $1.07 per square metre per annum.

### Section 7-914. Proposed scheduled rate

It is proposed that, with effect from 1 July 2026, the scheduled rate for Class AC-134 be 0.4650 per cent.

### Section 7-915. Proposed interest rate

It is proposed that the rate of interest under section 12-020 be 6.00 per cent per annum for the whole of the levy year.

### Section 7-916. Proposed scheduled rate

It is proposed that, with effect from 1 July 2026, the scheduled rate for Class AC-159 be 0.8550 per cent.

## Part 8. Exemptions and Reliefs

### Section 8-001. General

(1) This Part provides exemptions and reliefs that are applied in computing levy under Parts 2 to 6.

### Section 8-010. Statutory bodies

(1) Section 4-030 exempts statutory bodies from payroll levy. No other levy is reduced by reason only that a filer is a statutory body.

### Section 8-020. Establishment levy of public-class filers

(1) The establishment levy of each establishment of a public-class filer is computed at 60 per cent of the rate that would otherwise apply.

(2) The rate under subsection (1) is applied to each line before rounding, so that the line is the floor area, multiplied by the rate, multiplied by the active days in the period, multiplied by 60, divided by 365 and by 100, and rounded under section 1-080.

### Section 8-030. Relief for flood-affected premises

(1) A schedule about relief for flood-affected premises may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 13-086. The registrar of filers is not required to give reasons for a decision about relief for flood-affected premises unless an authorised officer asks for them through the filer portal within ninety days after the decision.

(2) Where the Commissioner so requires, the Registrar of Filers may give the Commissioner a application concerning relief for flood-affected premises, and the application must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within forty-two days after the relevant event. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about relief for flood-affected premises, the Review Tribunal may proceed on the information available, and section 8-001 applies accordingly. A register entry about relief for flood-affected premises may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 8-001.

(9) A relief under this section is granted by separate determination and is not reflected in an assessment statement.

### Section 8-040. Relief determined by the Commissioner in cases of hardship

(1) A declaration about relief determined by the Commissioner in cases of hardship may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-086. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to relief determined by the Commissioner in cases of hardship includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(2) Where the Commissioner so requires, a record about relief determined by the Commissioner in cases of hardship must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form. The commissioner must keep a declaration of every decision about relief determined by the Commissioner in cases of hardship for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Levy Collection Office on request through the filer portal.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Levy Collection Office may give a registered agent a record concerning relief determined by the Commissioner in cases of hardship, and the record must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-one days after the relevant event. The validity of a schedule about relief determined by the Commissioner in cases of hardship is not affected by a failure to comply with section 8-001 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The registrar of filers is not required to give reasons for a decision about relief determined by the Commissioner in cases of hardship unless the public officer of the filer asks for them in the approved electronic form within ninety days after the decision. The registrar of filers must keep a certificate of every decision about relief determined by the Commissioner in cases of hardship for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Levy Collection Office on request by registered post.

(9) A relief under this section is granted by separate determination and is not reflected in an assessment statement.

### Section 8-050. The relationship between reliefs

(1) The validity of a statement about the relationship between reliefs is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-086 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The validity of a register entry about the relationship between reliefs is not affected by a failure to comply with section 8-001 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(2) An authorised officer is not required to give reasons for a decision about the relationship between reliefs unless a registered agent asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-eight days after the decision. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a declaration relating to the relationship between reliefs that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a record relating to the relationship between reliefs that is not given within thirty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies.

(3) The registrar of filers must keep a record of every decision about the relationship between reliefs for not less than seven years, and must produce it to an authorised officer on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner. Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about the relationship between reliefs, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 13-086 applies accordingly. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a declaration relating to the relationship between reliefs that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies. The validity of a application about the relationship between reliefs is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-086 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(4) Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, the Levy Collection Office may give the Review Tribunal a return batch concerning the relationship between reliefs, and the return batch must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-eight days after the relevant event. For the purposes of this Division, a register entry about the relationship between reliefs must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing. The levy collection office is not required to give reasons for a decision about the relationship between reliefs unless a registered agent asks for them by registered post within thirty days after the decision.

(9) A relief under this section is granted by separate determination and is not reflected in an assessment statement.

## Part 9. Credits

### Section 9-001. Global limit

(1) The total of all amounts of credits applied for a filer for the levy year, including carried-forward amounts, must not exceed 75 per cent of its Gross Levy.

(2) The global limit is a single amount rounded under section 1-080.

### Section 9-002. Order of application

(1) Credits are applied in sequence under section 1-086, whatever Part of this Code the provision creating a credit appears in.

### Section 9-003. Application of a credit

(1) In its turn, each amount of a credit is applied up to the least of: the amount available; the remaining limit of that credit for the levy year; the Gross Levy remaining after credits already applied; and the remaining global limit.

(2) Credits are not refundable, and no credit reduces levy below nil.

### Section 9-004. Current-year amount

(1) The current-year amount of a credit that arises on claim is computed on the total qualifying expenditure claimed by the filer under that credit's code, and rounded under section 1-080.

(2) The current-year amount of a credit that arises without a claim is computed on the base stated in the provision creating it.

### Section 9-005. Limits of credits

(1) A limit stated as a percentage of a levy amount is that percentage of the amount, rounded under section 1-080, and applies to the total applied under the credit for the levy year, including carried-forward amounts.

### Section 9-006. Eligibility

(1) Eligibility for the current-year amount of a credit is determined on the facts of the levy year.

(2) Where a filer is not eligible for a credit, a claim under that credit has no effect, and the Commissioner must record that the claim was disregarded because the filer is not eligible.

### Section 9-007. Unused amounts

(1) Part 10 governs the carrying forward of unused amounts of credits.

### Section 9-104. Public Benefit Credit

(a) This section establishes the Public Benefit Credit, identified by credit code K-05.

(b) Eligibility. A filer is eligible if its filer class is public.

(c) Amount. The current-year amount of the credit is 50 per cent of the filer's qualifying expenditure under code K-05.

(d) Limit. The amount of the credit applied must not exceed 30 per cent of the filer's Gross Levy.

(e) Carry-forward period. The credit has no carry-forward period, and any unused amount lapses.

### Section 9-106. The certification of claims

(1) Subject to this Division, a statement relating to the certification of claims that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies. Subject to this Division, a reference in this Division to the certification of claims includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. A statement about the certification of claims may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 13-064.

(2) The registrar of filers is not required to give reasons for a decision about the certification of claims unless the Review Tribunal asks for them in writing within twenty-eight days after the decision. The validity of a register entry about the certification of claims is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-064 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(3) Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to the certification of claims includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to the certification of claims includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

### Section 9-109. Cooperative Development Credit

(a) This section establishes the Cooperative Development Credit, identified by credit code K-07.

(b) Eligibility. A filer is eligible if its filer class is mutual or its entity form is a cooperative society.

(c) Amount. The current-year amount of the credit is 25 per cent of the filer's qualifying expenditure under code K-07.

(d) Limit. The amount of the credit applied must not exceed 15 per cent of the filer's Primary Levy.

(e) Carry-forward period. An unused amount of the credit may be carried forward for three levy years.

### Section 9-112. Workforce Training Credit

(a) This section establishes the Workforce Training Credit, identified by credit code K-09.

(b) Eligibility. A filer is eligible if it is a filer.

(c) Amount. The current-year amount of the credit is 18 per cent of the filer's qualifying expenditure under code K-09.

(d) Limit. The amount of the credit applied must not exceed 12 per cent of the filer's Gross Levy.

(e) Carry-forward period. An unused amount of the credit may be carried forward for two levy years.

### Section 9-115. The evidence required for claims

(1) Subject to this Division, a reference in this Division to the evidence required for claims includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a reference in this Division to the evidence required for claims includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(2) Except where this Division otherwise provides, a return batch about the evidence required for claims must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing. A registered agent must keep a notice of every decision about the evidence required for claims for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Commissioner on request in writing. Nothing in section 13-064 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about the evidence required for claims where the particulars already given are incomplete. Nothing in section 9-004 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the evidence required for claims where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(3) Nothing in section 13-064 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about the evidence required for claims where the particulars already given are incomplete. Nothing in section 13-064 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the evidence required for claims where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(4) Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about the evidence required for claims, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-064 applies accordingly. A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about the evidence required for claims unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them by registered post within ninety days after the decision. Nothing in section 9-004 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about the evidence required for claims where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 9-118. Regional Employment Credit

(a) This section establishes the Regional Employment Credit, identified by credit code K-02.

(b) Eligibility. A filer is eligible if at least one of its establishments that has an active day in the levy year is located in an Enterprise Development Zone district.

(c) Amount. The current-year amount of the credit is 10 per cent of the filer's qualifying expenditure under code K-02.

(d) Limit. The amount of the credit applied must not exceed 20 per cent of the filer's Gross Levy.

(e) Carry-forward period. An unused amount of the credit may be carried forward for four levy years.

### Section 9-121. The amendment of claims

(1) The levy collection office must keep a notice of every decision about the amendment of claims for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request through the filer portal. Nothing in section 9-004 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about the amendment of claims where the particulars already given are incomplete. Nothing in section 9-004 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about the amendment of claims where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(2) For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to the amendment of claims includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about the amendment of claims, the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 13-064 applies accordingly.

(3) Except where this Division otherwise provides, the Levy Collection Office may give the Commissioner a declaration concerning the amendment of claims, and the declaration must be given in the approved electronic form within sixty days after the relevant event. Where the Commissioner so requires, a register entry relating to the amendment of claims that is not given within thirty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies.

### Section 9-125. Heritage Premises Credit

(a) This section establishes the Heritage Premises Credit, identified by credit code K-06.

(b) Eligibility. A filer is eligible if it is a filer.

(c) Amount. The current-year amount of the credit is 15 per cent of the filer's qualifying expenditure under code K-06.

(d) Limit. The amount of the credit applied must not exceed $4,000.

(e) Carry-forward period. An unused amount of the credit may be carried forward for two levy years.

### Section 9-128. The withdrawal of claims

(1) A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about the withdrawal of claims unless the Commissioner asks for them in writing within twenty-eight days after the decision. The levy collection office is not required to give reasons for a decision about the withdrawal of claims unless the Review Tribunal asks for them in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the decision.

(2) Nothing in section 13-064 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about the withdrawal of claims where the particulars already given are incomplete. Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about the withdrawal of claims, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 13-064 applies accordingly.

(3) The validity of a certificate about the withdrawal of claims is not affected by a failure to comply with section 9-004 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Nothing in section 13-064 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about the withdrawal of claims where the particulars already given are incomplete. The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about the withdrawal of claims unless a registered agent asks for them through the filer portal within sixty days after the decision.

(4) Except where this Division otherwise provides, a reference in this Division to the withdrawal of claims includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Subject to this Division, an authorised officer may give the Commissioner a return batch concerning the withdrawal of claims, and the return batch must be given by registered post within twenty-eight days after the relevant event.

(5) Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about the withdrawal of claims, the Review Tribunal may proceed on the information available, and section 13-064 applies accordingly. A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about the withdrawal of claims unless the Commissioner asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-eight days after the decision. Nothing in section 13-064 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about the withdrawal of claims where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 9-131. Research and Development Credit

(a) This section establishes the Research and Development Credit, identified by credit code K-04.

(b) Eligibility. A filer is eligible if its filer class is commercial and its entity form is a company.

(c) Amount. The current-year amount of the credit is 35 per cent of the filer's qualifying expenditure under code K-04.

(d) Limit. The amount of the credit applied must not exceed 25 per cent of the filer's Primary Levy.

(e) Carry-forward period. An unused amount of the credit may be carried forward for five levy years.

### Section 9-135. The audit of credit claims

(1) Except where this Division otherwise provides, a reference in this Division to the audit of credit claims includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. The validity of a notice about the audit of credit claims is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-064 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(2) Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a declaration relating to the audit of credit claims that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Levy Collection Office so certifies. The validity of a notice about the audit of credit claims is not affected by a failure to comply with section 9-004 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Subject to this Division, a statement relating to the audit of credit claims that is not given within fourteen days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies.

(3) A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about the audit of credit claims unless an authorised officer asks for them in the approved electronic form within twenty-eight days after the decision. The validity of a application about the audit of credit claims is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-064 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

### Section 9-140. Small Enterprise Investment Credit

(a) This section establishes the Small Enterprise Investment Credit, identified by credit code K-01.

(b) Eligibility. A filer is eligible if its activity levy is computed under section 2-040.

(c) Amount. The current-year amount of the credit is 20 per cent of the filer's qualifying expenditure under code K-01.

(d) Limit. The amount of the credit applied must not exceed $2,500.

(e) Carry-forward period. An unused amount of the credit may be carried forward for one levy years.

### Section 9-143. The register of approved programmes

(1) Nothing in section 13-064 prevents an authorised officer from requiring further particulars about the register of approved programmes where the particulars already given are incomplete. The validity of a declaration about the register of approved programmes is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-064 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to the register of approved programmes includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. The public officer of the filer is not required to give reasons for a decision about the register of approved programmes unless an authorised officer asks for them in writing within forty-two days after the decision.

(2) Where the Commissioner so requires, a record relating to the register of approved programmes that is not given within fourteen days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies. A notice about the register of approved programmes may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-064.

(3) The validity of a schedule about the register of approved programmes is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-064 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. A statement about the register of approved programmes may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 9-004. A record about the register of approved programmes may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-064.

(4) For the purposes of this Division, a declaration relating to the register of approved programmes that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies. For the purposes of this Division, the Levy Collection Office may give the Review Tribunal a statement concerning the register of approved programmes, and the statement must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within forty-two days after the relevant event. The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about the register of approved programmes unless an authorised officer asks for them by registered post within fourteen days after the decision. Where the Levy Collection Office fails to comply with a requirement about the register of approved programmes, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 9-004 applies accordingly.

(5) A record about the register of approved programmes may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of ninety days mentioned in section 9-004. Where the Commissioner so requires, the Commissioner may give the public officer of the filer a register entry concerning the register of approved programmes, and the register entry must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within forty-two days after the relevant event.

### Section 9-147. Rural Premises Credit

(a) This section establishes the Rural Premises Credit, identified by credit code K-10.

(b) Eligibility. A filer is eligible if its principal district is an Enterprise Development Zone district and is not a Coastal Zone district.

(c) Amount. The current-year amount of the credit is 20 per cent of the filer's qualifying expenditure under code K-10.

(d) Limit. The amount of the credit applied must not exceed $3,000.

(e) Carry-forward period. An unused amount of the credit may be carried forward for one levy years.

### Section 9-150. The cancellation of credits obtained by fraud

(1) Except where this Division otherwise provides, a schedule relating to the cancellation of credits obtained by fraud that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies. A schedule about the cancellation of credits obtained by fraud may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 9-004.

(2) Subject to this Division, a registered agent may give the Registrar of Filers a declaration concerning the cancellation of credits obtained by fraud, and the declaration must be given through the filer portal within fourteen days after the relevant event. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a record relating to the cancellation of credits obtained by fraud that is not given within twenty-one days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies.

(3) The registrar of filers must keep a return batch of every decision about the cancellation of credits obtained by fraud for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request through the filer portal. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, an authorised officer may give the public officer of the filer a schedule concerning the cancellation of credits obtained by fraud, and the schedule must be given in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the relevant event. Where the Commissioner so requires, the public officer of the filer may give the Registrar of Filers a notice concerning the cancellation of credits obtained by fraud, and the notice must be given in the approved electronic form within twenty-eight days after the relevant event. The registrar of filers must keep a application of every decision about the cancellation of credits obtained by fraud for not less than seven years, and must produce it to an authorised officer on request in the approved electronic form.

(4) Subject to this Division, a return batch about the cancellation of credits obtained by fraud must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. Nothing in section 9-004 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about the cancellation of credits obtained by fraud where the particulars already given are incomplete. A return batch about the cancellation of credits obtained by fraud may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-064. The validity of a schedule about the cancellation of credits obtained by fraud is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-064 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

### Section 9-152. Export Development Credit

(a) This section establishes the Export Development Credit, identified by credit code K-08. The credit arises without a claim.

(b) Eligibility. A filer is eligible if its predominant class is a Band II or Band III class.

(c) Amount. The current-year amount of the credit is 0.80 per cent of the total export receipts of all lines of the filer, whether or not those export receipts are excluded from assessable receipts.

(d) Limit. The amount of the credit applied must not exceed 5 per cent of the filer's Gross Levy.

(e) Carry-forward period. The credit has no carry-forward period, and any unused amount lapses.

## Part 10. Carry-forward of Credits

### Section 10-001. Carried-forward amounts

(1) A carried-forward amount is applied in the turn of the credit to which it relates under section 9-002.

### Section 10-002. Order within a credit

(1) Within the turn of a credit, carried-forward amounts are applied before the current-year amount, in ascending order of their years of origin.

(2) The limit of the credit applies to the total of the carried-forward amounts and the current-year amount applied.

### Section 10-003. Period of carry-forward

(1) An amount of a credit originating in a levy year may be applied in each of the levy years following that year, up to the number of levy years of the credit's carry-forward period.

(2) A carried-forward amount whose period has ended before the levy year is expired, has no effect, and the Commissioner must record that it was disregarded as expired.

### Section 10-004. Credits without a carry-forward period

(1) A carried-forward amount recorded under the code of a credit that has no carry-forward period, or under a code that does not identify a credit, has no effect, and the Commissioner must record that it was disregarded as not permitted.

(2) Section 10-003(2) does not apply to an amount to which subsection (1) applies.

### Section 10-005. Unused amounts at the end of the levy year

(1) The part of a carried-forward amount or of a current-year amount that is not applied is carried forward to the next levy year, with its original year of origin, if that next levy year is within its carry-forward period.

(2) The current-year amount of the levy year originates in the levy year 2026.

(3) Any other unused part lapses.

### Section 10-006. Eligibility in the year of application

(1) A carried-forward amount may be applied only if the filer is eligible for the credit in the levy year in which it is applied.

### Section 10-010. The carry-forward register

(1) A schedule about the carry-forward register may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 13-061. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to the carry-forward register includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Commissioner may give an authorised officer a record concerning the carry-forward register, and the record must be given in the approved electronic form within forty-two days after the relevant event. Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about the carry-forward register, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 10-001 applies accordingly.

(3) Nothing in section 10-001 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the carry-forward register where the particulars already given are incomplete. Where the Commissioner fails to comply with a requirement about the carry-forward register, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-061 applies accordingly. Where the public officer of the filer fails to comply with a requirement about the carry-forward register, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-061 applies accordingly.

## Part 11. Minimum Levy

### Section 11-001. Top-up

(1) Where the Gross Levy of a filer less the credits applied is less than its minimum levy, a top-up equal to the difference is added.

### Section 11-010. Amount of minimum levy

(1) The minimum levy is: for an individual carrying on business as a sole trader, $50; for a partnership, $120; for a company, $300; for a trust, $180; for a cooperative society, $90; for a statutory body, $0.

### Section 11-020. Records of minimum levy

(1) Subject to this Division, a certificate about the publication of minimum levy amounts must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal. Except where this Division otherwise provides, the Registrar of Filers may give an authorised officer a declaration concerning the publication of minimum levy amounts, and the declaration must be given in writing within thirty days after the relevant event.

(2) A schedule about the publication of minimum levy amounts may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of twenty-eight days mentioned in section 11-010. For the purposes of this Division, a notice about the publication of minimum levy amounts must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. The registrar of filers must keep a schedule of every decision about the publication of minimum levy amounts for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request through the filer portal. For the purposes of this Division, a record about the publication of minimum levy amounts must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

## Part 12. Instalments, Payment and Interest

### Section 12-001. Instalments

(1) Net Levy is payable in four instalments, one for each quarter.

(2) Each of the first three instalments is one quarter of Net Levy rounded down to the whole cent, and the fourth instalment is the remainder.

### Section 12-005. Due dates

(1) The instalment for a quarter is due on the last day of the month following the end of the quarter.

(2) Section 1-090(2) applies to every due date.

### Section 12-010. Payments taken into account

(1) Payments are taken into account in the order of their payment dates and, for payments of the same date, in ascending order of payment identifier.

(2) A payment dated after the assessment date is not taken into account, and the Commissioner must record that the payment was disregarded.

### Section 12-012. Allocation of payments

(1) Each payment is allocated to the earliest instalment that has an unpaid amount, and any remaining part of the payment to the next such instalment, and so on.

(2) Any part of a payment that exceeds every unpaid instalment remains as a credit balance.

### Section 12-020. Rate of interest

(1) Interest accrues on late instalments at the rate of 7.00 per cent per annum.

### Section 12-030. Accrual of interest

(1) Interest accrues separately on each part of an instalment allocated from a payment made after the due date, for each day after the due date up to and including the payment date.

(2) Interest accrues on any part of an instalment that is unpaid at the assessment date for each day after the due date up to and including the assessment date.

(3) The interest for a day is the part of the instalment multiplied by the rate in force on that day and divided by 365.

(4) The interest on each part is a single amount.

### Section 12-040. Rounding of interest

(1) Notwithstanding section 1-080, the interest on each part of an instalment is rounded to the nearest whole cent, and an amount exactly half-way between two whole cents is rounded up.

### Section 12-050. Balance

(1) The balance of a filer is its Net Levy plus interest, less the payments taken into account.

### Section 12-060. Payment methods

(1) The validity of a return batch about methods of payment is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-034 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The levy collection office must keep a register entry of every decision about methods of payment for not less than seven years, and must produce it to an authorised officer on request in writing. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a statement about methods of payment must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal. Where the Commissioner so requires, the public officer of the filer may give the Commissioner a record concerning methods of payment, and the record must be given in the approved electronic form within sixty days after the relevant event.

(2) A schedule about methods of payment may be varied by a registered agent at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 12-010. Nothing in section 13-034 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about methods of payment where the particulars already given are incomplete. Subject to this Division, a declaration about methods of payment must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post.

(3) Except where this Division otherwise provides, a record relating to methods of payment that is not given within twenty-one days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies. A return batch about methods of payment may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-034.

## Part 13. Administration and Procedure

### Section 13-001. Administration

(1) The Commissioner administers this Code.

### Section 13-002. Authorised officers

(1) Nothing in section 13-001 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about the appointment of authorised officers where the particulars already given are incomplete. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about the appointment of authorised officers, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 13-001 applies accordingly. The levy collection office is not required to give reasons for a decision about the appointment of authorised officers unless a registered agent asks for them in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the decision. The validity of a application about the appointment of authorised officers is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-001 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(2) A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about the appointment of authorised officers unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them in writing within thirty days after the decision. A register entry about the appointment of authorised officers may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-001.

(3) Nothing in section 13-001 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about the appointment of authorised officers where the particulars already given are incomplete. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a reference in this Division to the appointment of authorised officers includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Nothing in section 13-001 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the appointment of authorised officers where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 13-003. Assessment statements

(1) The Commissioner prepares an assessment statement for every filer in a return batch in the form published under section 13-050.

(2) Late lodgement penalties, fees and reliefs granted by separate determination are not included in an assessment statement.

### Section 13-004. The registration of filers

(1) The validity of a application about the registration of filers is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-004 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. A application about the registration of filers may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-007.

(2) The registrar of filers must keep a register entry of every decision about the registration of filers for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Levy Collection Office on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner. For the purposes of this Division, a record about the registration of filers must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form. Nothing in section 13-015 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about the registration of filers where the particulars already given are incomplete. A declaration about the registration of filers may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-012.

(3) A return batch about the registration of filers may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-012. The validity of a notice about the registration of filers is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-010 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(4) Where the Commissioner so requires, a statement about the registration of filers must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing. The commissioner must keep a declaration of every decision about the registration of filers for not less than seven years, and must produce it to an authorised officer on request by registered post. The validity of a schedule about the registration of filers is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-009 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(5) For the purposes of this Division, a declaration about the registration of filers must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. A notice about the registration of filers may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 13-010. The validity of a certificate about the registration of filers is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-013 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. For the purposes of this Division, the Registrar of Filers may give the Levy Collection Office a notice concerning the registration of filers, and the notice must be given in writing within forty-two days after the relevant event.

(6) Nothing in section 13-006 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about the registration of filers where the particulars already given are incomplete. Where the Commissioner fails to comply with a requirement about the registration of filers, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-012 applies accordingly.

### Section 13-005. Changes to registered particulars

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a statement about changes to registered particulars must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. Nothing in section 13-007 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about changes to registered particulars where the particulars already given are incomplete. A return batch about changes to registered particulars may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 13-009.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a record relating to changes to registered particulars that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies. A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about changes to registered particulars unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within thirty days after the decision. The registrar of filers must keep a statement of every decision about changes to registered particulars for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Review Tribunal on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner. Subject to this Division, a register entry relating to changes to registered particulars that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies.

(3) A certificate about changes to registered particulars may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-014. A return batch about changes to registered particulars may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-012. A certificate about changes to registered particulars may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 13-005. A certificate about changes to registered particulars may be varied by a registered agent at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-008.

### Section 13-006. The notification of cessation

(1) The public officer of the filer must keep a schedule of every decision about the notification of cessation for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Review Tribunal on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner. Nothing in section 13-007 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the notification of cessation where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(2) Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a application relating to the notification of cessation that is not given within twenty-one days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies. Nothing in section 13-014 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about the notification of cessation where the particulars already given are incomplete. Nothing in section 13-011 prevents an authorised officer from requiring further particulars about the notification of cessation where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(3) Nothing in section 13-009 prevents an authorised officer from requiring further particulars about the notification of cessation where the particulars already given are incomplete. The registrar of filers is not required to give reasons for a decision about the notification of cessation unless a registered agent asks for them in writing within fourteen days after the decision. The levy collection office must keep a schedule of every decision about the notification of cessation for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request in writing. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to the notification of cessation includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

### Section 13-007. The appointment of registered agents

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a return batch relating to the appointment of registered agents that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies. Nothing in section 13-013 prevents an authorised officer from requiring further particulars about the appointment of registered agents where the particulars already given are incomplete. The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about the appointment of registered agents unless a registered agent asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-one days after the decision. Subject to this Division, a registered agent may give an authorised officer a declaration concerning the appointment of registered agents, and the declaration must be given by registered post within thirty days after the relevant event.

(2) Except where this Division otherwise provides, the public officer of the filer may give the Levy Collection Office a record concerning the appointment of registered agents, and the record must be given through the filer portal within twenty-one days after the relevant event. Except where this Division otherwise provides, the Levy Collection Office may give the Review Tribunal a certificate concerning the appointment of registered agents, and the certificate must be given in the approved electronic form within twenty-eight days after the relevant event. Subject to this Division, a register entry relating to the appointment of registered agents that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to the appointment of registered agents includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(3) A certificate about the appointment of registered agents may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-005. A register entry about the appointment of registered agents may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of ninety days mentioned in section 13-015.

(4) Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to the appointment of registered agents includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. For the purposes of this Division, a certificate about the appointment of registered agents must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

(5) Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about the appointment of registered agents, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-009 applies accordingly. Subject to this Division, a notice about the appointment of registered agents must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

(6) Nothing in section 13-015 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the appointment of registered agents where the particulars already given are incomplete. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a reference in this Division to the appointment of registered agents includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

### Section 13-008. The revocation of an agency

(1) The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about the revocation of an agency unless an authorised officer asks for them in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the decision. A registered agent must keep a certificate of every decision about the revocation of an agency for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Review Tribunal on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a schedule relating to the revocation of an agency that is not given within twenty-one days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies.

(2) Nothing in section 13-007 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about the revocation of an agency where the particulars already given are incomplete. Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about the revocation of an agency, the Review Tribunal may proceed on the information available, and section 13-004 applies accordingly. Where the Commissioner fails to comply with a requirement about the revocation of an agency, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 13-013 applies accordingly.

(3) Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about the revocation of an agency, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-015 applies accordingly. A schedule about the revocation of an agency may be varied by a registered agent at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 13-015.

(4) Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, an authorised officer may give the Review Tribunal a record concerning the revocation of an agency, and the record must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-one days after the relevant event. The commissioner must keep a statement of every decision about the revocation of an agency for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request in the approved electronic form. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a declaration about the revocation of an agency must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing.

(5) Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about the revocation of an agency, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 13-013 applies accordingly. Subject to this Division, a reference in this Division to the revocation of an agency includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(6) Nothing in section 13-012 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about the revocation of an agency where the particulars already given are incomplete. Nothing in section 13-012 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the revocation of an agency where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 13-009. Electronic lodgement of return batches

(1) For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to electronic lodgement of return batches includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. The levy collection office must keep a certificate of every decision about electronic lodgement of return batches for not less than seven years, and must produce it to an authorised officer on request through the filer portal. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about electronic lodgement of return batches, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 13-013 applies accordingly.

(2) A schedule about electronic lodgement of return batches may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of twenty-eight days mentioned in section 13-011. Subject to this Division, a notice relating to electronic lodgement of return batches that is not given within twenty-one days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a certificate relating to electronic lodgement of return batches that is not given within twenty-one days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies.

(3) Where the Commissioner so requires, the Levy Collection Office may give the Commissioner a statement concerning electronic lodgement of return batches, and the statement must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-one days after the relevant event. Subject to this Division, a reference in this Division to electronic lodgement of return batches includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(4) The public officer of the filer must keep a statement of every decision about electronic lodgement of return batches for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Registrar of Filers on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner. The validity of a register entry about electronic lodgement of return batches is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-015 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(5) For the purposes of this Division, the Review Tribunal may give the Registrar of Filers a application concerning electronic lodgement of return batches, and the application must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within thirty days after the relevant event. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a record about electronic lodgement of return batches must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal. Nothing in section 13-006 prevents an authorised officer from requiring further particulars about electronic lodgement of return batches where the particulars already given are incomplete. Subject to this Division, a application relating to electronic lodgement of return batches that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Levy Collection Office so certifies.

### Section 13-010. Lodgement on paper

(1) A schedule about lodgement on paper may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 13-009. A schedule about lodgement on paper may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of ninety days mentioned in section 13-006.

(2) The review tribunal must keep a notice of every decision about lodgement on paper for not less than seven years, and must produce it to an authorised officer on request in writing. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to lodgement on paper includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. The validity of a return batch about lodgement on paper is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-010 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(3) The validity of a application about lodgement on paper is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-007 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about lodgement on paper, the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 13-004 applies accordingly. The levy collection office must keep a statement of every decision about lodgement on paper for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request through the filer portal. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to lodgement on paper includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(4) Nothing in section 13-010 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about lodgement on paper where the particulars already given are incomplete. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about lodgement on paper, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-004 applies accordingly. A statement about lodgement on paper may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-012. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a statement relating to lodgement on paper that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies.

(5) For the purposes of this Division, the Review Tribunal may give the Registrar of Filers a notice concerning lodgement on paper, and the notice must be given through the filer portal within fourteen days after the relevant event. Subject to this Division, a statement relating to lodgement on paper that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies.

### Section 13-011. The content of batch manifests

(1) A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about the content of batch manifests unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the decision. The public officer of the filer must keep a declaration of every decision about the content of batch manifests for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request in writing.

(2) A notice about the content of batch manifests may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-006. Nothing in section 13-015 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about the content of batch manifests where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(3) For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to the content of batch manifests includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Subject to this Division, a return batch relating to the content of batch manifests that is not given within fourteen days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Levy Collection Office so certifies. A return batch about the content of batch manifests may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-014.

(4) A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about the content of batch manifests unless an authorised officer asks for them in the approved electronic form within sixty days after the decision. A notice about the content of batch manifests may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-004. Subject to this Division, the Review Tribunal may give the public officer of the filer a return batch concerning the content of batch manifests, and the return batch must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within sixty days after the relevant event. Subject to this Division, a certificate relating to the content of batch manifests that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies.

### Section 13-012. The issue of assessment statements

(1) The validity of a declaration about the issue of assessment statements is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-009 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Nothing in section 13-007 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about the issue of assessment statements where the particulars already given are incomplete. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about the issue of assessment statements, the Review Tribunal may proceed on the information available, and section 13-011 applies accordingly. A certificate about the issue of assessment statements may be varied by a registered agent at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-005.

(2) A schedule about the issue of assessment statements may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-009. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to the issue of assessment statements includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(3) Nothing in section 13-005 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about the issue of assessment statements where the particulars already given are incomplete. The review tribunal must keep a declaration of every decision about the issue of assessment statements for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request in the approved electronic form. Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about the issue of assessment statements, the Review Tribunal may proceed on the information available, and section 13-010 applies accordingly. Where the Commissioner so requires, a register entry relating to the issue of assessment statements that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies.

(1) Where the Commissioner so requires, a register entry relating to the service of notices that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a reference in this Division to the service of notices includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. The validity of a notice about the service of notices is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-015 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about the service of notices unless the public officer of the filer asks for them in writing within sixty days after the decision.

(2) Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a registered agent may give the Review Tribunal a statement concerning the service of notices, and the statement must be given through the filer portal within sixty days after the relevant event. Where the Levy Collection Office fails to comply with a requirement about the service of notices, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-004 applies accordingly.

(3) An authorised officer must keep a declaration of every decision about the service of notices for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request through the filer portal. Nothing in section 13-005 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the service of notices where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(4) The levy collection office must keep a declaration of every decision about the service of notices for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request by registered post. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to the service of notices includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(5) The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about the service of notices unless a registered agent asks for them by registered post within forty-two days after the decision. The review tribunal must keep a notice of every decision about the service of notices for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request through the filer portal. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a declaration about the service of notices must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a statement relating to the service of notices that is not given within fourteen days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies.

### Section 13-042. Electronic service of notices

(1) The validity of a schedule about electronic service of notices is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-009 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about electronic service of notices, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-014 applies accordingly. A registered agent must keep a register entry of every decision about electronic service of notices for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Registrar of Filers on request in writing.
