(2) Where the Commissioner so requires, the Registrar of Filers may give an authorised officer a record concerning electronic service of notices, and the record must be given through the filer portal within twenty-eight days after the relevant event. Nothing in section 13-014 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about electronic service of notices where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(3) Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a record relating to electronic service of notices that is not given within twenty-one days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a return batch relating to electronic service of notices that is not given within fourteen days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies.

(4) For the purposes of this Division, a register entry about electronic service of notices must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about electronic service of notices, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-010 applies accordingly. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to electronic service of notices includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(5) The public officer of the filer is not required to give reasons for a decision about electronic service of notices unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within thirty days after the decision. Where the Commissioner so requires, the Review Tribunal may give the Registrar of Filers a notice concerning electronic service of notices, and the notice must be given in the approved electronic form within ninety days after the relevant event.

(6) The registrar of filers must keep a return batch of every decision about electronic service of notices for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Review Tribunal on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner. Except where this Division otherwise provides, the Review Tribunal may give a registered agent a schedule concerning electronic service of notices, and the schedule must be given in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the relevant event. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, the Commissioner may give the Review Tribunal a declaration concerning electronic service of notices, and the declaration must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-eight days after the relevant event. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, the Levy Collection Office may give the public officer of the filer a notice concerning electronic service of notices, and the notice must be given in writing within forty-two days after the relevant event.

### Section 13-043. The computation of periods of time

(1) A declaration about the computation of periods of time may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-009. Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about the computation of periods of time, the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 13-004 applies accordingly.

(2) Subject to this Division, the Commissioner may give the Levy Collection Office a record concerning the computation of periods of time, and the record must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-eight days after the relevant event. A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about the computation of periods of time unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them in the approved electronic form within twenty-eight days after the decision.

(3) Where the public officer of the filer fails to comply with a requirement about the computation of periods of time, the Review Tribunal may proceed on the information available, and section 13-014 applies accordingly. The validity of a application about the computation of periods of time is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-009 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about the computation of periods of time unless the Review Tribunal asks for them in the approved electronic form within twenty-one days after the decision. The commissioner must keep a record of every decision about the computation of periods of time for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Registrar of Filers on request through the filer portal.

### Section 13-044. The public officer of a company

(1) Except where this Division otherwise provides, a schedule relating to the public officer of a company that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies. The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about the public officer of a company unless the Review Tribunal asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within sixty days after the decision.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a record about the public officer of a company must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. For the purposes of this Division, a notice relating to the public officer of a company that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies. Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about the public officer of a company, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-014 applies accordingly.

(3) Where the Commissioner fails to comply with a requirement about the public officer of a company, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 13-013 applies accordingly. Nothing in section 13-013 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about the public officer of a company where the particulars already given are incomplete. Subject to this Division, a reference in this Division to the public officer of a company includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Subject to this Division, a return batch relating to the public officer of a company that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Commissioner may give an authorised officer a certificate concerning the public officer of a company, and the certificate must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within ninety days after the relevant event. Where the public officer of the filer fails to comply with a requirement about the public officer of a company, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 13-008 applies accordingly. Nothing in section 13-004 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the public officer of a company where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 13-045. The translation of documents

(1) The validity of a register entry about the translation of documents is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-007 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Where the Commissioner so requires, a registered agent may give an authorised officer a declaration concerning the translation of documents, and the declaration must be given in the approved electronic form within sixty days after the relevant event. A record about the translation of documents may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-012.

(2) Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about the translation of documents, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-009 applies accordingly. A notice about the translation of documents may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-015.

(3) Except where this Division otherwise provides, a reference in this Division to the translation of documents includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Nothing in section 13-004 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about the translation of documents where the particulars already given are incomplete. Where the Commissioner fails to comply with a requirement about the translation of documents, the Review Tribunal may proceed on the information available, and section 13-010 applies accordingly.

(4) Where the Commissioner so requires, the Levy Collection Office may give the Registrar of Filers a return batch concerning the translation of documents, and the return batch must be given in the approved electronic form within twenty-one days after the relevant event. A register entry about the translation of documents may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-012. For the purposes of this Division, the Review Tribunal may give a registered agent a register entry concerning the translation of documents, and the register entry must be given in writing within forty-two days after the relevant event. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a registered agent may give an authorised officer a statement concerning the translation of documents, and the statement must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-eight days after the relevant event.

### Section 13-046. The publication of rulings

(1) For the purposes of this Division, a return batch about the publication of rulings must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. Where the public officer of the filer fails to comply with a requirement about the publication of rulings, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 13-006 applies accordingly. A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about the publication of rulings unless the Commissioner asks for them by registered post within ninety days after the decision. Subject to this Division, a record about the publication of rulings must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing.

(2) Where the public officer of the filer fails to comply with a requirement about the publication of rulings, the Registrar of Filers may proceed on the information available, and section 13-009 applies accordingly. Subject to this Division, the Registrar of Filers may give a registered agent a notice concerning the publication of rulings, and the notice must be given in writing within forty-two days after the relevant event. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to the publication of rulings includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(3) A register entry about the publication of rulings may be varied by a registered agent at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-015. For the purposes of this Division, a certificate about the publication of rulings must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. Subject to this Division, the public officer of the filer may give the Registrar of Filers a declaration concerning the publication of rulings, and the declaration must be given in the approved electronic form within fourteen days after the relevant event. A registered agent must keep a record of every decision about the publication of rulings for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Commissioner on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

(4) For the purposes of this Division, the Registrar of Filers may give the public officer of the filer a schedule concerning the publication of rulings, and the schedule must be given by registered post within sixty days after the relevant event. The validity of a return batch about the publication of rulings is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-006 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(5) Where the Commissioner fails to comply with a requirement about the publication of rulings, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-008 applies accordingly. The validity of a return batch about the publication of rulings is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-013 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(6) Nothing in section 13-006 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about the publication of rulings where the particulars already given are incomplete. The public officer of the filer must keep a register entry of every decision about the publication of rulings for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request through the filer portal.

### Section 13-047. Advance opinions

(1) For the purposes of this Division, the Commissioner may give the Registrar of Filers a notice concerning advance opinions, and the notice must be given through the filer portal within forty-two days after the relevant event. A application about advance opinions may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of twenty-eight days mentioned in section 13-004. For the purposes of this Division, a declaration relating to advance opinions that is not given within thirty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies. A record about advance opinions may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 13-011.

(2) The validity of a application about advance opinions is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-009 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. A notice about advance opinions may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-015. The validity of a application about advance opinions is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-008 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Nothing in section 13-006 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about advance opinions where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(3) Nothing in section 13-009 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about advance opinions where the particulars already given are incomplete. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a registered agent may give the Levy Collection Office a certificate concerning advance opinions, and the certificate must be given through the filer portal within fourteen days after the relevant event. A certificate about advance opinions may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-010. Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to advance opinions includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

### Section 13-048. Settlements of disputes

(1) Where the public officer of the filer fails to comply with a requirement about settlements of disputes, the Registrar of Filers may proceed on the information available, and section 13-005 applies accordingly. Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to settlements of disputes includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(2) A schedule about settlements of disputes may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 13-012. A application about settlements of disputes may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-010. Subject to this Division, a statement about settlements of disputes must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form.

(3) The registrar of filers must keep a certificate of every decision about settlements of disputes for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Commissioner on request by registered post. For the purposes of this Division, a schedule about settlements of disputes must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a record about settlements of disputes must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal.

### Section 13-049. The compromise of levy debts

(1) A return batch about the compromise of levy debts may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-011. Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about the compromise of levy debts, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-011 applies accordingly. For the purposes of this Division, the Levy Collection Office may give the Review Tribunal a declaration concerning the compromise of levy debts, and the declaration must be given through the filer portal within twenty-one days after the relevant event. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a record about the compromise of levy debts must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

(2) Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to the compromise of levy debts includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Nothing in section 13-014 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about the compromise of levy debts where the particulars already given are incomplete. Subject to this Division, a reference in this Division to the compromise of levy debts includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(3) Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about the compromise of levy debts, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 13-009 applies accordingly. Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about the compromise of levy debts, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-015 applies accordingly. The validity of a schedule about the compromise of levy debts is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-005 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(4) Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about the compromise of levy debts, the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 13-005 applies accordingly. For the purposes of this Division, a application relating to the compromise of levy debts that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies. The registrar of filers must keep a notice of every decision about the compromise of levy debts for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

(5) Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about the compromise of levy debts, the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 13-011 applies accordingly. Where the Commissioner so requires, a statement relating to the compromise of levy debts that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies. Nothing in section 13-014 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about the compromise of levy debts where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 13-050. The procedure for refunds

(1) Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, the Commissioner may give a registered agent a declaration concerning the procedure for refunds, and the declaration must be given in writing within twenty-one days after the relevant event. For the purposes of this Division, a notice about the procedure for refunds must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing.

(2) The levy collection office is not required to give reasons for a decision about the procedure for refunds unless a registered agent asks for them by registered post within twenty-eight days after the decision. For the purposes of this Division, a schedule relating to the procedure for refunds that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Levy Collection Office so certifies. Where the Commissioner so requires, a certificate about the procedure for refunds must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal. The levy collection office is not required to give reasons for a decision about the procedure for refunds unless the Commissioner asks for them in writing within sixty days after the decision.

(3) Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about the procedure for refunds, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 13-006 applies accordingly. An authorised officer must keep a statement of every decision about the procedure for refunds for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request through the filer portal. Nothing in section 13-011 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about the procedure for refunds where the particulars already given are incomplete. The registrar of filers is not required to give reasons for a decision about the procedure for refunds unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them by registered post within ninety days after the decision.

(4) Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to the procedure for refunds includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Subject to this Division, the Review Tribunal may give the Levy Collection Office a certificate concerning the procedure for refunds, and the certificate must be given through the filer portal within thirty days after the relevant event.

### Section 13-051. Set-off against other Commonwealth debts

(1) Except where this Division otherwise provides, a reference in this Division to set-off against other Commonwealth debts includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Subject to this Division, a certificate about set-off against other Commonwealth debts must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

(2) Where the Commissioner so requires, the Registrar of Filers may give the Commissioner a application concerning set-off against other Commonwealth debts, and the application must be given in writing within twenty-one days after the relevant event. For the purposes of this Division, a record about set-off against other Commonwealth debts must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal. The validity of a statement about set-off against other Commonwealth debts is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-006 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(3) A schedule about set-off against other Commonwealth debts may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 13-005. Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about set-off against other Commonwealth debts, the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 13-010 applies accordingly. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a record relating to set-off against other Commonwealth debts that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies.

(4) A statement about set-off against other Commonwealth debts may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of twenty-eight days mentioned in section 13-008. A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about set-off against other Commonwealth debts unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them through the filer portal within fourteen days after the decision. Where the Levy Collection Office fails to comply with a requirement about set-off against other Commonwealth debts, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-012 applies accordingly. The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about set-off against other Commonwealth debts unless the Commissioner asks for them in writing within twenty-eight days after the decision.

(5) Nothing in section 13-005 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about set-off against other Commonwealth debts where the particulars already given are incomplete. The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about set-off against other Commonwealth debts unless the public officer of the filer asks for them through the filer portal within fourteen days after the decision.

### Section 13-052. The write-off of irrecoverable amounts

(1) The levy collection office must keep a statement of every decision about the write-off of irrecoverable amounts for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request by registered post. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a reference in this Division to the write-off of irrecoverable amounts includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(2) Nothing in section 13-015 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about the write-off of irrecoverable amounts where the particulars already given are incomplete. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to the write-off of irrecoverable amounts includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Where the Commissioner so requires, a certificate relating to the write-off of irrecoverable amounts that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies.

(3) The registrar of filers is not required to give reasons for a decision about the write-off of irrecoverable amounts unless the Commissioner asks for them through the filer portal within sixty days after the decision. A return batch about the write-off of irrecoverable amounts may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-013.

### Section 13-053. The delegation of powers

(1) Where the Levy Collection Office fails to comply with a requirement about the delegation of powers, the Review Tribunal may proceed on the information available, and section 13-014 applies accordingly. The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about the delegation of powers unless a registered agent asks for them in writing within fourteen days after the decision.

(2) Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about the delegation of powers, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-012 applies accordingly. For the purposes of this Division, a application relating to the delegation of powers that is not given within thirty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies. Nothing in section 13-012 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about the delegation of powers where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(3) The registrar of filers must keep a certificate of every decision about the delegation of powers for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request by registered post. Subject to this Division, a reference in this Division to the delegation of powers includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about the delegation of powers unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them by registered post within forty-two days after the decision.

(4) The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about the delegation of powers unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them through the filer portal within forty-two days after the decision. The validity of a notice about the delegation of powers is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-007 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about the delegation of powers, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 13-011 applies accordingly.

(5) Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a declaration about the delegation of powers must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. The validity of a register entry about the delegation of powers is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-013 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Subject to this Division, a application relating to the delegation of powers that is not given within twenty-one days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies.

(6) For the purposes of this Division, an authorised officer may give the public officer of the filer a declaration concerning the delegation of powers, and the declaration must be given through the filer portal within ninety days after the relevant event. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, the Commissioner may give the Registrar of Filers a schedule concerning the delegation of powers, and the schedule must be given through the filer portal within thirty days after the relevant event. Subject to this Division, a return batch relating to the delegation of powers that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies.

### Section 13-054. The annual report of the Commissioner

(1) The validity of a declaration about the annual report of the Commissioner is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-014 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The levy collection office is not required to give reasons for a decision about the annual report of the Commissioner unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them through the filer portal within twenty-one days after the decision.

(2) A registered agent must keep a declaration of every decision about the annual report of the Commissioner for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Commissioner on request in the approved electronic form. For the purposes of this Division, an authorised officer may give the public officer of the filer a schedule concerning the annual report of the Commissioner, and the schedule must be given in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the relevant event. A application about the annual report of the Commissioner may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-006. The validity of a certificate about the annual report of the Commissioner is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-005 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(3) A declaration about the annual report of the Commissioner may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-013. A schedule about the annual report of the Commissioner may be varied by a registered agent at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-012. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a declaration about the annual report of the Commissioner must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

(4) An authorised officer is not required to give reasons for a decision about the annual report of the Commissioner unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them by registered post within thirty days after the decision. Subject to this Division, a declaration about the annual report of the Commissioner must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post.

(5) A statement about the annual report of the Commissioner may be varied by a registered agent at any time before the close of the period of ninety days mentioned in section 13-006. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about the annual report of the Commissioner, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 13-010 applies accordingly. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to the annual report of the Commissioner includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(6) A record about the annual report of the Commissioner may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-007. The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about the annual report of the Commissioner unless a registered agent asks for them by registered post within fourteen days after the decision. Where the Commissioner so requires, the Commissioner may give the Levy Collection Office a schedule concerning the annual report of the Commissioner, and the schedule must be given by registered post within twenty-one days after the relevant event. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a application about the annual report of the Commissioner must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post.

### Section 13-055. The approval of forms

(1) A schedule about the approval of forms may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of twenty-eight days mentioned in section 13-006. Nothing in section 13-015 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the approval of forms where the particulars already given are incomplete. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a register entry about the approval of forms must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form.

(2) Nothing in section 13-010 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about the approval of forms where the particulars already given are incomplete. Where the Commissioner so requires, a statement relating to the approval of forms that is not given within fourteen days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies.

(3) Except where this Division otherwise provides, a notice about the approval of forms must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. The public officer of the filer is not required to give reasons for a decision about the approval of forms unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them by registered post within thirty days after the decision. The registrar of filers is not required to give reasons for a decision about the approval of forms unless the Commissioner asks for them through the filer portal within twenty-one days after the decision.

(4) Nothing in section 13-010 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about the approval of forms where the particulars already given are incomplete. The validity of a register entry about the approval of forms is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-009 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Where the Levy Collection Office fails to comply with a requirement about the approval of forms, the Registrar of Filers may proceed on the information available, and section 13-010 applies accordingly. Nothing in section 13-015 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about the approval of forms where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(5) The levy collection office must keep a record of every decision about the approval of forms for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request in the approved electronic form. An authorised officer must keep a certificate of every decision about the approval of forms for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request through the filer portal.

(6) A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about the approval of forms unless an authorised officer asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within forty-two days after the decision. The validity of a certificate about the approval of forms is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-014 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The validity of a declaration about the approval of forms is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-010 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

### Section 13-056. The payment of fees

(1) Except where this Division otherwise provides, a reference in this Division to the payment of fees includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. A notice about the payment of fees may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-005.

(2) Where the Commissioner so requires, a schedule relating to the payment of fees that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies. For the purposes of this Division, the Review Tribunal may give the Levy Collection Office a record concerning the payment of fees, and the record must be given by registered post within ninety days after the relevant event. Subject to this Division, a notice relating to the payment of fees that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies. An authorised officer is not required to give reasons for a decision about the payment of fees unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them by registered post within thirty days after the decision.

(3) A statement about the payment of fees may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of twenty-eight days mentioned in section 13-010. An authorised officer must keep a notice of every decision about the payment of fees for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Levy Collection Office on request in writing. A declaration about the payment of fees may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of twenty-eight days mentioned in section 13-010. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a declaration about the payment of fees must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

(4) The public officer of the filer must keep a statement of every decision about the payment of fees for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Levy Collection Office on request in the approved electronic form. Nothing in section 13-009 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about the payment of fees where the particulars already given are incomplete. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, the Review Tribunal may give an authorised officer a register entry concerning the payment of fees, and the register entry must be given by registered post within fourteen days after the relevant event.

(5) Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a declaration about the payment of fees must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. A register entry about the payment of fees may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-012.

### Section 13-057. Evidentiary certificates

(1) Where the Levy Collection Office fails to comply with a requirement about evidentiary certificates, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 13-009 applies accordingly. The validity of a statement about evidentiary certificates is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-014 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The validity of a record about evidentiary certificates is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-010 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a registered agent may give the public officer of the filer a certificate concerning evidentiary certificates, and the certificate must be given by registered post within thirty days after the relevant event.

(2) Where the Commissioner so requires, the Levy Collection Office may give the Review Tribunal a schedule concerning evidentiary certificates, and the schedule must be given in writing within fourteen days after the relevant event. The commissioner must keep a notice of every decision about evidentiary certificates for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Levy Collection Office on request by registered post. Nothing in section 13-014 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about evidentiary certificates where the particulars already given are incomplete. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a schedule relating to evidentiary certificates that is not given within thirty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies.

(3) An authorised officer is not required to give reasons for a decision about evidentiary certificates unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them in writing within thirty days after the decision. Nothing in section 13-008 prevents an authorised officer from requiring further particulars about evidentiary certificates where the particulars already given are incomplete. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to evidentiary certificates includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about evidentiary certificates, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 13-010 applies accordingly.

(4) The commissioner must keep a application of every decision about evidentiary certificates for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request in writing. Where the Commissioner fails to comply with a requirement about evidentiary certificates, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 13-013 applies accordingly.

(5) Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about evidentiary certificates, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-007 applies accordingly. Nothing in section 13-009 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about evidentiary certificates where the particulars already given are incomplete. Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about evidentiary certificates, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-005 applies accordingly.

(6) A schedule about evidentiary certificates may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-012. The review tribunal must keep a return batch of every decision about evidentiary certificates for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request in the approved electronic form.

### Section 13-058. The Boundaries Register

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a declaration about the Boundaries Register must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. A registered agent must keep a declaration of every decision about the Boundaries Register for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Registrar of Filers on request through the filer portal.

(2) Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, the Registrar of Filers may give the public officer of the filer a declaration concerning the Boundaries Register, and the declaration must be given in the approved electronic form within ninety days after the relevant event. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a application about the Boundaries Register must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about the Boundaries Register, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 13-015 applies accordingly.

(3) For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to the Boundaries Register includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. For the purposes of this Division, the Commissioner may give a registered agent a declaration concerning the Boundaries Register, and the declaration must be given in the approved electronic form within twenty-eight days after the relevant event.

### Section 13-059. The register of filers

(1) A schedule about the register of filers may be varied by a registered agent at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-007. The validity of a schedule about the register of filers is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-004 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The commissioner must keep a register entry of every decision about the register of filers for not less than seven years, and must produce it to a registered agent on request in writing. The validity of a certificate about the register of filers is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-011 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(2) The public officer of the filer is not required to give reasons for a decision about the register of filers unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them by registered post within ninety days after the decision. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a statement about the register of filers must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner. For the purposes of this Division, the Review Tribunal may give the Commissioner a certificate concerning the register of filers, and the certificate must be given in the approved electronic form within twenty-one days after the relevant event.

(3) Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about the register of filers, the Registrar of Filers may proceed on the information available, and section 13-012 applies accordingly. Subject to this Division, a register entry about the register of filers must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated through the filer portal. The validity of a certificate about the register of filers is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-007 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a statement relating to the register of filers that is not given within fourteen days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Levy Collection Office so certifies. The levy collection office is not required to give reasons for a decision about the register of filers unless an authorised officer asks for them in writing within thirty days after the decision. Nothing in section 13-007 prevents an authorised officer from requiring further particulars about the register of filers where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 13-060. The register of establishments

(1) Nothing in section 13-009 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about the register of establishments where the particulars already given are incomplete. Nothing in section 13-008 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about the register of establishments where the particulars already given are incomplete. The validity of a declaration about the register of establishments is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-006 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(2) The validity of a application about the register of establishments is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-011 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Nothing in section 13-011 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about the register of establishments where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(3) A declaration about the register of establishments may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-008. Where the Commissioner so requires, a register entry relating to the register of establishments that is not given within fourteen days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to the register of establishments includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(4) An authorised officer must keep a declaration of every decision about the register of establishments for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Review Tribunal on request in writing. Where the Commissioner so requires, a register entry relating to the register of establishments that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the public officer of the filer so certifies. Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about the register of establishments, the Registrar of Filers may proceed on the information available, and section 13-005 applies accordingly.

### Section 13-061. The carry-forward register

(1) An authorised officer must keep a schedule of every decision about the carry-forward register for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Registrar of Filers on request in writing. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a record about the carry-forward register must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated by registered post. A schedule about the carry-forward register may be varied by a registered agent at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-008. A register entry about the carry-forward register may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of forty-two days mentioned in section 13-013.

(2) A record about the carry-forward register may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-004. Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about the carry-forward register, the Registrar of Filers may proceed on the information available, and section 13-007 applies accordingly. Nothing in section 13-014 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about the carry-forward register where the particulars already given are incomplete. Nothing in section 13-011 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about the carry-forward register where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(3) The validity of a schedule about the carry-forward register is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-011 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Subject to this Division, a register entry about the carry-forward register must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

(4) Nothing in section 13-011 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the carry-forward register where the particulars already given are incomplete. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a notice relating to the carry-forward register that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Registrar of Filers so certifies. Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to the carry-forward register includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about the carry-forward register unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within thirty days after the decision.

(5) The validity of a register entry about the carry-forward register is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-005 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. A schedule about the carry-forward register may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-005. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a certificate about the carry-forward register must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

### Section 13-062. The payment register

(1) Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about the payment register, the Registrar of Filers may proceed on the information available, and section 13-004 applies accordingly. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to the payment register includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Nothing in section 13-004 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about the payment register where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(2) Where the Commissioner so requires, a register entry relating to the payment register that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies. A record about the payment register may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-011. For the purposes of this Division, the Levy Collection Office may give the public officer of the filer a application concerning the payment register, and the application must be given in the approved electronic form within fourteen days after the relevant event.

(3) The validity of a record about the payment register is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-009 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The validity of a schedule about the payment register is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-004 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. A register entry about the payment register may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-010.

(4) Nothing in section 13-009 prevents the Levy Collection Office from requiring further particulars about the payment register where the particulars already given are incomplete. The levy collection office must keep a record of every decision about the payment register for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request through the filer portal. Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to the payment register includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Nothing in section 13-012 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the payment register where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 13-063. The claim register

(1) Subject to this Division, the Commissioner may give the Levy Collection Office a application concerning the claim register, and the application must be given in writing within twenty-eight days after the relevant event. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a reference in this Division to the claim register includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about the claim register, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-004 applies accordingly.

(2) A record about the claim register may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of twenty-eight days mentioned in section 13-011. A statement about the claim register may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of thirty days mentioned in section 13-009. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a statement relating to the claim register that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies.

(3) The commissioner must keep a certificate of every decision about the claim register for not less than seven years, and must produce it to an authorised officer on request in writing. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a record about the claim register must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing.

(4) Subject to this Division, the Review Tribunal may give an authorised officer a notice concerning the claim register, and the notice must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-one days after the relevant event. For the purposes of this Division, a registered agent may give the Commissioner a certificate concerning the claim register, and the certificate must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within twenty-eight days after the relevant event. Where the Commissioner so requires, a declaration about the claim register must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form.

(5) Nothing in section 13-012 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about the claim register where the particulars already given are incomplete. A certificate about the claim register may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-010. A statement about the claim register may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-007. Where the Commissioner so requires, a schedule about the claim register must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form.

### Section 13-064. The correction of clerical errors in registers

(1) Nothing in section 13-008 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about the correction of clerical errors in registers where the particulars already given are incomplete. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to the correction of clerical errors in registers includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to the correction of clerical errors in registers includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(2) Except where this Division otherwise provides, an authorised officer may give the public officer of the filer a register entry concerning the correction of clerical errors in registers, and the register entry must be given in the approved electronic form within ninety days after the relevant event. Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about the correction of clerical errors in registers, the Levy Collection Office may proceed on the information available, and section 13-013 applies accordingly. Where the Commissioner fails to comply with a requirement about the correction of clerical errors in registers, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-008 applies accordingly.

(3) The registrar of filers is not required to give reasons for a decision about the correction of clerical errors in registers unless the Review Tribunal asks for them through the filer portal within thirty days after the decision. The validity of a schedule about the correction of clerical errors in registers is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-015 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(4) The validity of a return batch about the correction of clerical errors in registers is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-013 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. The validity of a notice about the correction of clerical errors in registers is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-013 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(5) Nothing in section 13-009 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the correction of clerical errors in registers where the particulars already given are incomplete. A statement about the correction of clerical errors in registers may be varied by the Registrar of Filers at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-012. A schedule about the correction of clerical errors in registers may be varied by the public officer of the filer at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-011. A register entry about the correction of clerical errors in registers may be varied by the Commissioner at any time before the close of the period of fourteen days mentioned in section 13-004.

### Section 13-065. The certification of floor areas

(1) A record about the certification of floor areas may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-011. Where the Levy Collection Office fails to comply with a requirement about the certification of floor areas, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-007 applies accordingly.

(2) Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to the certification of floor areas includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to the certification of floor areas includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Where the Commissioner so requires, a notice relating to the certification of floor areas that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if an authorised officer so certifies.

(3) Notwithstanding section 6-020, for the levy year 2026 the coastal supplement is 1.5000 per cent.

(4) Nothing in section 13-014 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about the certification of floor areas where the particulars already given are incomplete. The validity of a application about the certification of floor areas is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-009 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice.

(5) Except where this Division otherwise provides, a record relating to the certification of floor areas that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies. The validity of a schedule about the certification of floor areas is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-010 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about the certification of floor areas, the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 13-011 applies accordingly. Subject to this Division, a reference in this Division to the certification of floor areas includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(6) Where the Levy Collection Office fails to comply with a requirement about the certification of floor areas, the Registrar of Filers may proceed on the information available, and section 13-010 applies accordingly. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Levy Collection Office may give an authorised officer a statement concerning the certification of floor areas, and the statement must be given in writing within thirty days after the relevant event. Nothing in section 13-009 prevents the Registrar of Filers from requiring further particulars about the certification of floor areas where the particulars already given are incomplete. A declaration about the certification of floor areas may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-010.

### Section 13-066. The certification of energy meters

(1) Nothing in section 13-013 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about the certification of energy meters where the particulars already given are incomplete. For the purposes of this Division, a statement relating to the certification of energy meters that is not given within fourteen days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies.

(2) A registered agent is not required to give reasons for a decision about the certification of energy meters unless the Commissioner asks for them in the approved electronic form within ninety days after the decision. The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about the certification of energy meters unless the public officer of the filer asks for them through the filer portal within ninety days after the decision.

(3) The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about the certification of energy meters unless the Registrar of Filers asks for them in the manner approved by the Commissioner within thirty days after the decision. The commissioner must keep a return batch of every decision about the certification of energy meters for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Registrar of Filers on request in writing.

### Section 13-067. The certification of apprenticeship contracts

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Commissioner may give the Registrar of Filers a record concerning the certification of apprenticeship contracts, and the record must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within ninety days after the relevant event. Nothing in section 13-006 prevents the public officer of the filer from requiring further particulars about the certification of apprenticeship contracts where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(2) The validity of a register entry about the certification of apprenticeship contracts is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-008 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to the certification of apprenticeship contracts includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. The commissioner must keep a application of every decision about the certification of apprenticeship contracts for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Levy Collection Office on request through the filer portal. The registrar of filers must keep a schedule of every decision about the certification of apprenticeship contracts for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the public officer of the filer on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner.

(3) Subject to this Division, a application about the certification of apprenticeship contracts must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in the approved electronic form. Where the Levy Collection Office fails to comply with a requirement about the certification of apprenticeship contracts, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 13-015 applies accordingly. Nothing in section 13-011 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about the certification of apprenticeship contracts where the particulars already given are incomplete. Where the Commissioner so requires, a statement relating to the certification of apprenticeship contracts that is not given within thirty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Levy Collection Office so certifies.

(4) For the purposes of this Division, a register entry relating to the certification of apprenticeship contracts that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies. A certificate about the certification of apprenticeship contracts may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of twenty-one days mentioned in section 13-006.

### Section 13-068. The heritage premises register

(1) A registered agent must keep a return batch of every decision about the heritage premises register for not less than seven years, and must produce it to an authorised officer on request in writing. The public officer of the filer must keep a certificate of every decision about the heritage premises register for not less than seven years, and must produce it to an authorised officer on request in writing.

(2) Where an authorised officer fails to comply with a requirement about the heritage premises register, the Review Tribunal may proceed on the information available, and section 13-005 applies accordingly. The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about the heritage premises register unless the Review Tribunal asks for them by registered post within thirty days after the decision. Where the Commissioner so requires, the Commissioner may give the Registrar of Filers a notice concerning the heritage premises register, and the notice must be given in the manner approved by the Commissioner within ninety days after the relevant event.

(3) The validity of a schedule about the heritage premises register is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-004 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to the heritage premises register includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. A application about the heritage premises register may be varied by the Review Tribunal at any time before the close of the period of ninety days mentioned in section 13-004. Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to the heritage premises register includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

### Section 13-069. The accreditation of research programmes

(1) Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, a reference in this Division to the accreditation of research programmes includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about the accreditation of research programmes, an authorised officer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-015 applies accordingly. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a record about the accreditation of research programmes must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing.

(2) Where the Commissioner fails to comply with a requirement about the accreditation of research programmes, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 13-004 applies accordingly. Unless the Commissioner otherwise directs, the Registrar of Filers may give the Levy Collection Office a record concerning the accreditation of research programmes, and the record must be given through the filer portal within sixty days after the relevant event. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a declaration relating to the accreditation of research programmes that is not given within forty-two days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a certificate relating to the accreditation of research programmes that is not given within twenty-eight days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if a registered agent so certifies. Where the Commissioner so requires, a application relating to the accreditation of research programmes that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Commissioner so certifies. The validity of a notice about the accreditation of research programmes is not affected by a failure to comply with section 13-013 unless the failure causes substantial prejudice. For the purposes of this Division, a application relating to the accreditation of research programmes that is not given within ninety days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Review Tribunal so certifies.

(4) An authorised officer is not required to give reasons for a decision about the accreditation of research programmes unless the Levy Collection Office asks for them in the approved electronic form within sixty days after the decision. Where the Registrar of Filers fails to comply with a requirement about the accreditation of research programmes, the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 13-012 applies accordingly. For the purposes of this Division, a application about the accreditation of research programmes must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing.

(5) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may give a registered agent a notice concerning the accreditation of research programmes, and the notice must be given by registered post within sixty days after the relevant event. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a reference in this Division to the accreditation of research programmes includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it.

(6) Where the Commissioner so requires, a reference in this Division to the accreditation of research programmes includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Where the Levy Collection Office fails to comply with a requirement about the accreditation of research programmes, a registered agent may proceed on the information available, and section 13-009 applies accordingly. Where the Commissioner fails to comply with a requirement about the accreditation of research programmes, the Review Tribunal may proceed on the information available, and section 13-015 applies accordingly. Nothing in section 13-010 prevents the Review Tribunal from requiring further particulars about the accreditation of research programmes where the particulars already given are incomplete.

### Section 13-070. The conduct of audits

(1) For the purposes of this Division, a declaration relating to the conduct of audits that is not given within fourteen days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Levy Collection Office so certifies. A registered agent must keep a notice of every decision about the conduct of audits for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Commissioner on request in the manner approved by the Commissioner. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to the conduct of audits includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Subject to this Division, a certificate about the conduct of audits must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing.

(2) A schedule about the conduct of audits may be varied by the Levy Collection Office at any time before the close of the period of twenty-eight days mentioned in section 13-015. A certificate about the conduct of audits may be varied by an authorised officer at any time before the close of the period of sixty days mentioned in section 13-008. The review tribunal must keep a schedule of every decision about the conduct of audits for not less than seven years, and must produce it to the Commissioner on request through the filer portal. Nothing in section 13-013 prevents the Commissioner from requiring further particulars about the conduct of audits where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(3) The review tribunal is not required to give reasons for a decision about the conduct of audits unless the Commissioner asks for them through the filer portal within twenty-one days after the decision. Except where this Division otherwise provides, a certificate about the conduct of audits must identify the filer by its filer identifier and must be signed or authenticated in writing.

(4) Where a registered agent fails to comply with a requirement about the conduct of audits, the public officer of the filer may proceed on the information available, and section 13-008 applies accordingly. For the purposes of this Division, a reference in this Division to the conduct of audits includes a reference to any matter that is incidental or ancillary to it. Nothing in section 13-004 prevents a registered agent from requiring further particulars about the conduct of audits where the particulars already given are incomplete.

(5) The commissioner is not required to give reasons for a decision about the conduct of audits unless the public officer of the filer asks for them through the filer portal within ninety days after the decision. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a record relating to the conduct of audits that is not given within sixty days is taken to have been given on the last day of that period only if the Levy Collection Office so certifies. Except where this Division otherwise provides, the Commissioner may give a registered agent a return batch concerning the conduct of audits, and the return batch must be given by registered post within twenty-one days after the relevant event. Where the Review Tribunal fails to comply with a requirement about the conduct of audits, the Commissioner may proceed on the information available, and section 13-015 applies accordingly.

### Section 13-071. The selection of filers for audit
